359:     fn assistant_start(&mut self) {
360:         let reuse = self.entries.last().is_some_and(
361:             |entry| matches!(entry, Entry::Assistant { text, .. } if text.trim().is_empty()),
362:         );
363:         if reuse {
364:             self.streaming = Some(self.entries.len() - 1);
365:             self.scroll = 0;
366:             return;
367:         }
368:         self.entries.push(Entry::Assistant {
369:             text: String::new(),
370:             thinking: String::new(),
371:             thinking_open: false,
372:         });
373:         self.streaming = Some(self.entries.len() - 1);
374:         self.scroll = 0;
375:     }
376: 
377:     fn assistant_delta(&mut self, delta: &str) {
378:         if self.streaming.is_none() {
379:             self.assistant_start();
380:         }
381:         if let Some(index) = self.streaming {
382:             if let Some(Entry::Assistant { text, .. }) = self.entries.get_mut(index) {
383:                 text.push_str(delta);
384:             }
385:         }
386:         self.scroll = 0;
387:     }
388: 
389:     fn thinking_start(&mut self) {
390:         if self.streaming.is_none() {
391:             self.assistant_start();
392:         }
393:         if let Some(index) = self.streaming {
394:             if let Some(Entry::Assistant { thinking_open, .. }) = self.entries.get_mut(index) {
395:                 *thinking_open = true;
396:             }
397:         }
398:         self.scroll = 0;
399:     }
400: 
401:     fn thinking_delta(&mut self, delta: &str) {
402:         self.thinking_start();
403:         if let Some(index) = self.streaming {
404:             if let Some(Entry::Assistant { thinking, .. }) = self.entries.get_mut(index) {
405:                 thinking.push_str(delta);
406:             }
407:         }
408:     }
409: 
410:     fn thinking_final(&mut self, text: String) {
411:         if self.streaming.is_none() && text.is_empty() {
412:             return;
413:         }
414:         self.thinking_start();
415:         if let Some(index) = self.streaming {
416:             if let Some(Entry::Assistant {
417:                 thinking,
418:                 thinking_open,
419:                 ..
420:             }) = self.entries.get_mut(index)
421:             {
422:                 if !text.is_empty() {
423:                     *thinking = text;
424:                 }
425:                 *thinking_open = false;
426:             }
427:         }
428:     }
429: 
430:     fn assistant_final(&mut self, text: String) {
431:         if let Some(index) = self.streaming.take() {
432:             if let Some(Entry::Assistant {
433:                 text: existing,
434:                 thinking_open,
435:                 ..
436:             }) = self.entries.get_mut(index)
437:             {
438:                 *existing = text;
439:                 *thinking_open = false;
440:             }
441:         } else if !text.is_empty() {
442:             self.entries.push(Entry::Assistant {
443:                 text,
444:                 thinking: String::new(),
445:                 thinking_open: false,
446:             });
447:         }
448:     }
449: 
450:     fn toggle_thinking(&mut self, index: usize) -> bool {
451:         let Some(Entry::Assistant {
452:             thinking,
453:             thinking_open,
454:             ..
455:         }) = self.entries.get_mut(index)
456:         else {
457:             return false;
458:         };
459:         if thinking.trim().is_empty() {
460:             return false;
461:         }
462:         *thinking_open = !*thinking_open;
463:         true
464:     }
465: 
466:     fn push_tool(&mut self, id: &str, label: String, state: ToolState, body: Vec<ToolBodyLine>) {
467:         let index = self.entries.len();
468:         self.entries
469:             .push(Entry::Tool(ToolEntry { label, state, body }));
470:         self.tools.insert(id.to_string(), index);
471:         self.scroll = 0;
472:     }
473: 
474:     fn update_tool(&mut self, id: &str, state: ToolState) {
475:         if let Some(index) = self.tools.get(id).copied() {
476:             if let Some(Entry::Tool(tool)) = self.entries.get_mut(index) {
477:                 tool.state = state;
478:             }
479:         }
480:     }
481: 
482:     fn clear_input(&mut self) {
483:         self.input.clear();
484:         self.input_cursor = 0;
485:         self.clear_suggestions();
486:     }
487: 
488:     fn clear_suggestions(&mut self) {
489:         self.suggestions.clear();
490:         self.suggestion_selected = 0;
491:     }
492: 
493:     fn insert_input(&mut self, ch: char) {
494:         let mut chars: Vec<char> = self.input.chars().collect();
495:         let col = self.input_cursor.min(chars.len());
496:         chars.insert(col, ch);
497:         self.input = chars.into_iter().collect();
498:         self.input_cursor = col + 1;
499:     }
500: 
501:     fn backspace_input(&mut self) {
502:         if self.input_cursor == 0 {
503:             return;
504:         }
505:         let mut chars: Vec<char> = self.input.chars().collect();
506:         let col = self.input_cursor.min(chars.len());
507:         chars.remove(col - 1);
508:         self.input = chars.into_iter().collect();
509:         self.input_cursor = col - 1;
510:     }
511: 
512:     fn delete_input(&mut self) {
513:         let mut chars: Vec<char> = self.input.chars().collect();
514:         if self.input_cursor < chars.len() {
515:             chars.remove(self.input_cursor);
516:             self.input = chars.into_iter().collect();
517:         }
518:     }
519: 
520:     fn move_input(&mut self, delta: isize) {
521:         let length = self.input.chars().count();
522:         let next = self.input_cursor as isize + delta;
523:         self.input_cursor = next.clamp(0, length as isize) as usize;
524:     }
525: 
526:     fn input_home(&mut self) {
527:         self.input_cursor = 0;
528:     }
529: 
530:     fn input_end(&mut self) {
531:         self.input_cursor = self.input.chars().count();
532:     }
533: 
534:     fn reference_query(&self) -> Option<(usize, String)> {
535:         let chars: Vec<char> = self.input.chars().collect();
536:         let end = self.input_cursor.min(chars.len());
537:         let mut start = None;
538:         for index in (0..end).rev() {
539:             match chars[index] {
540:                 '@' => {
541:                     if index == 0 || chars[index - 1].is_whitespace() {
542:                         start = Some(index);
543:                     }
544:                     break;
545:                 }
546:                 ch if ch.is_whitespace() => break,
547:                 _ => {}
548:             }
549:         }
550:         let start = start?;
551:         Some((start, chars[start + 1..end].iter().collect()))
552:     }
553: 
554:     fn update_suggestions(&mut self, files: &[String]) {
555:         let Some((_, query)) = self.reference_query() else {
556:             self.clear_suggestions();
557:             return;
558:         };
559:         let query = query.to_lowercase();
560:         let mut matches: Vec<(u8, String)> = files
561:             .iter()
562:             .filter_map(|path| {
563:                 let lower = path.to_lowercase();
564:                 if !lower.contains(&query) {
565:                     return None;
566:                 }
567:                 let name = lower.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(&lower);
568:                 let rank = if query.is_empty() || name.starts_with(&query) {
569:                     0
570:                 } else if lower.starts_with(&query) {
571:                     1
572:                 } else if name.contains(&query) {
573:                     2
574:                 } else {
575:                     3
576:                 };
577:                 Some((rank, path.clone()))
578:             })
579:             .collect();
580:         matches.sort_by(|a, b| {
581:             a.0.cmp(&b.0)
582:                 .then_with(|| a.1.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.1.to_lowercase()))
583:         });
584:         self.suggestions = matches.into_iter().take(8).map(|(_, path)| path).collect();
585:         if self.suggestion_selected >= self.suggestions.len() {
586:             self.suggestion_selected = self.suggestions.len().saturating_sub(1);
587:         }
588:     }
589: 
590:     fn move_suggestion(&mut self, delta: isize) {
591:         if self.suggestions.is_empty() {
592:             return;
593:         }
594:         let next = self.suggestion_selected as isize + delta;
595:         self.suggestion_selected =
596:             next.clamp(0, self.suggestions.len().saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
597:     }
598: 
599:     fn accept_suggestion(&mut self) -> bool {
600:         if self.suggestions.is_empty() {
601:             return false;
602:         }
603:         let Some((start, _)) = self.reference_query() else {
604:             self.clear_suggestions();
605:             return false;
606:         };
607:         let index = self
608:             .suggestion_selected
609:             .min(self.suggestions.len().saturating_sub(1));
610:         let replacement = format!("@{} ", self.suggestions[index]);
611:         let mut chars: Vec<char> = self.input.chars().collect();
612:         let end = self.input_cursor.min(chars.len());
613:         chars.splice(start..end, replacement.chars());
614:         self.input = chars.into_iter().collect();
615:         self.input_cursor = start + replacement.chars().count();
616:         self.clear_suggestions();
617:         true
618:     }
619: 
620:     fn scroll_by(&mut self, delta: isize) {
621:         let next = self.scroll as isize + delta;
622:         self.scroll = next.max(0) as usize;
623:     }
624: 
625:     fn reset(&mut self) {
626:         self.entries.clear();
627:         self.clear_input();
628:         self.scroll = 0;
629:         self.busy = false;
630:         self.streaming = None;
631:         self.tools.clear();
632:     }
633: 
634:     fn last_assistant(&self) -> Option<String> {
635:         self.entries.iter().rev().find_map(|entry| match entry {
636:             Entry::Assistant { text, .. } if !text.trim().is_empty() => Some(text.clone()),
637:             _ => None,
638:         })
639:     }
640: }
641: 
642: struct PendingTool {
643:     kind: Option<TrackKind>,
644:     path: Option<PathBuf>,
645:     before: Option<String>,
646:     start: usize,
647:     end: usize,
648: }
649: 
650: pub struct App {
651:     pub root: PathBuf,
652:     pub tree: FileTree,
653:     pub project_files: Vec<String>,
654:     pub editor: Editor,
655:     pub agent: Agent,
656:     pub git: Git,
657:     pub pi: PiState,
658:     pub focus: Focus,
659:     pub sidebar: Sidebar,
660:     pub should_quit: bool,
661:     pub status: Option<(String, Instant)>,
662:     pub tracks: Vec<Track>,
663:     pub ui: UiRects,
664:     pub now: Instant,
665:     pub started_at: Instant,
666:     last_tree_refresh: Instant,
667:     pub model: Option<String>,
668:     pub diff: Option<DiffView>,
669:     pub menu: Option<CommandMenu>,
670:     pub context_menu: Option<ContextMenu>,
671:     pub dialog: Option<FileDialog>,
672:     pub unsupported: Option<PathBuf>,
673:     pub dragging: bool,
674:     pub agent_hidden: bool,
675:     pub left_width: Option<u16>,
676:     pub right_width: Option<u16>,
677:     pub resizing: Option<ResizeEdge>,
678:     pub hover: Option<(u16, u16)>,
679:     pub hover_edge: Option<ResizeEdge>,
680:     pub follow_editor_cursor: bool,
681:     last_tree_click: Option<(Instant, Option<usize>)>,
682:     pointer: PointerShape,
683:     pointer_dirty: bool,
684:     pub commands: Vec<PiCommand>,
685:     pub models: Vec<Value>,
686:     context_percent: Option<u64>,
687:     stats_notice: bool,
688:     auto_compacted: bool,
689:     pending: HashMap<String, PendingTool>,
690:     pi_stderr: Vec<String>,
691:     pi_exited: bool,
692:     project_lines: Option<Receiver<Vec<String>>>,
693: }
694: 
695: impl App {
696:     pub fn new(root: PathBuf, force_no_pi: bool) -> Self {
697:         let tree = FileTree::new(&root);
698:         let project_files = tree.files();
699:         let editor = Editor::new(&root);
700:         let git = Git::new(&root);
701:         let project_lines = if cfg!(test) {
702:             None
703:         } else {
704:             let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
705:             let scan_root = root.clone();
706:             thread::spawn(move || {
707:                 let lines = crate::predict::scan_project(&scan_root);
708:                 let _ = tx.send(lines);
709:             });
710:             Some(rx)
711:         };
712:         let pi_path = if force_no_pi { None } else { find_pi() };
713:         let pi = match pi_path.clone() {
714:             Some(path) => match PiClient::spawn(&path, &root) {
715:                 Ok(mut client) => {
716:                     let _ = client.get_state();
717:                     let _ = client.send(json!({ "type": "get_commands" }));
718:                     let _ = client.send(json!({ "type": "get_available_models" }));
719:                     let _ = client.send(json!({ "type": "set_auto_compaction", "enabled": false }));
720:                     let _ = client.send(json!({ "type": "get_session_stats" }));
721:                     PiState::Running(client)
722:                 }
723:                 Err(error) => PiState::Failed(format!("could not start pi: {error}")),
724:             },
725:             None => PiState::Missing,
726:         };
727: 
728:         let app = Self {
729:             root,
730:             tree,
731:             project_files,
732:             editor,
733:             agent: Agent::new(),
734:             git,
735:             pi,
736:             focus: Focus::Tree,
737:             sidebar: Sidebar::Files,
738:             should_quit: false,
739:             status: None,
740:             tracks: Vec::new(),
741:             ui: UiRects::default(),
742:             now: Instant::now(),
743:             started_at: Instant::now(),
744:             last_tree_refresh: Instant::now(),
745:             model: None,
746:             diff: None,
747:             menu: None,
748:             context_menu: None,
749:             dialog: None,
750:             unsupported: None,
751:             dragging: false,
752:             agent_hidden: false,
753:             left_width: None,
754:             right_width: None,
755:             resizing: None,
756:             hover: None,
757:             hover_edge: None,
758:             follow_editor_cursor: true,
759:             last_tree_click: None,
760:             pointer: PointerShape::Default,
761:             pointer_dirty: false,
762:             commands: Vec::new(),
763:             models: Vec::new(),
764:             context_percent: None,
765:             stats_notice: false,
766:             auto_compacted: false,
767:             pending: HashMap::new(),
768:             pi_stderr: Vec::new(),
769:             pi_exited: false,
770:             project_lines,
771:         };
772:         app
773:     }
774: 
775:     pub fn shutdown(&mut self) {
776:         if let PiState::Running(client) = &mut self.pi {
777:             client.shutdown();
778:         }
779:     }
780: 
781:     pub fn set_status(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) {
782:         self.status = Some((text.into(), Instant::now()));
783:     }
784: 
785:     pub fn drain_pi(&mut self) {
786:         let mut events = Vec::new();
787:         if let PiState::Running(client) = &self.pi {
788:             while let Ok(event) = client.rx.try_recv() {
789:                 events.push(event);
790:             }
791:         }
792: 
793:         for event in events {
794:             match event {
795:                 PiEvent::Record(value) => self.on_pi_record(value),
796:                 PiEvent::Stderr(line) => {
797:                     self.pi_stderr.push(line);
798:                     if self.pi_stderr.len() > 20 {
799:                         self.pi_stderr.remove(0);
800:                     }
801:                 }
802:                 PiEvent::Exit => self.pi_exited = true,
803:             }
804:         }
805: 
806:         if self.pi_exited && matches!(self.pi, PiState::Running(_)) {
807:             let detail = self
808:                 .pi_stderr
809:                 .last()
810:                 .cloned()
811:                 .unwrap_or_else(|| "pi exited".to_string());
812:             self.pi = PiState::Failed(detail.clone());
813:             self.agent.busy = false;
814:             self.agent.note(detail);
815:         }
816:     }
817: 
818:     fn on_pi_record(&mut self, value: Value) {
819:         let kind = value
820:             .get("type")
821:             .and_then(Value::as_str)
822:             .unwrap_or_default();
823:         match kind {
824:             "response" => self.on_response(&value),
825:             "message_start" => {
826:                 let role = value
827:                     .pointer("/message/role")
828:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
829:                     .unwrap_or_default();
830:                 if role == "assistant" {
831:                     self.agent.assistant_start();
832:                     self.agent.busy = true;
833:                 }
834:             }
835:             "message_update" => {
836:                 let event = value.get("assistantMessageEvent");
837:                 let event_type = event
838:                     .and_then(|event| event.get("type"))
839:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
840:                     .unwrap_or_default();
841:                 match event_type {
842:                     "text_delta" => {
843:                         if let Some(delta) = event
844:                             .and_then(|event| event.get("delta"))
845:                             .and_then(Value::as_str)
846:                         {
847:                             self.agent.assistant_delta(delta);
848:                         }
849:                     }
850:                     "thinking_start" => self.agent.thinking_start(),
851:                     "thinking_delta" => {
852:                         if let Some(delta) = event
853:                             .and_then(|event| event.get("delta"))
854:                             .and_then(Value::as_str)
855:                         {
856:                             self.agent.thinking_delta(delta);
857:                         }
858:                     }
859:                     "thinking_end" => {
860:                         if let Some(content) = event
861:                             .and_then(|event| event.get("content"))
862:                             .and_then(Value::as_str)
863:                         {
864:                             self.agent.thinking_final(content.to_string());
865:                         }
866:                     }
867:                     "error" => {
868:                         if let Some(error) = event
869:                             .and_then(|event| event.get("error"))
870:                             .and_then(Value::as_str)
871:                         {
872:                             self.agent.note(error.to_string());
873:                             self.agent.busy = false;
874:                         }
875:                     }
876:                     _ => {}
877:                 }
878:             }
879:             "message_end" => {
880:                 let role = value
881:                     .pointer("/message/role")
882:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
883:                     .unwrap_or_default();
884:                 if role == "assistant" {
885:                     let text = value
886:                         .pointer("/message")
887:                         .map(message_text)
888:                         .unwrap_or_default();
889:                     self.agent.assistant_final(text);
890:                 }
891:             }
892:             "tool_execution_start" => self.on_tool_start(&value),
893:             "tool_execution_end" => self.on_tool_end(&value),
894:             "agent_start" => self.agent.busy = true,
895:             "agent_settled" => {
896:                 self.agent.busy = false;
897:                 self.agent.scroll = 0;
898:                 self.request_context_stats(false);
899:             }
900:             "auto_retry_start" => {
901:                 let attempt = value.get("attempt").and_then(Value::as_u64).unwrap_or(0);
902:                 let max = value
903:                     .get("maxAttempts")
904:                     .and_then(Value::as_u64)
905:                     .unwrap_or(0);
906:                 let error = value
907:                     .get("errorMessage")
908:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
909:                     .unwrap_or_default();
910:                 self.agent.note(format!("retry {attempt}/{max} {error}"));
911:             }
912:             "extension_error" => {
913:                 if let Some(error) = value.get("error").and_then(Value::as_str) {
914:                     self.agent.note(error.to_string());
915:                 }
916:             }
917:             "compaction_start" => self.agent.note("compacting context"),
918:             "compaction_end" => {
919:                 if let Some(error) = value.get("errorMessage").and_then(Value::as_str) {
920:                     self.agent.note(format!("compaction failed: {error}"));
921:                 }
922:                 self.request_context_stats(false);
923:             }
924:             _ => {}
925:         }
926:     }
927: 
928:     fn on_response(&mut self, value: &Value) {
929:         let command = value
930:             .get("command")
931:             .and_then(Value::as_str)
932:             .unwrap_or_default();
933:         let success = value
934:             .get("success")
935:             .and_then(Value::as_bool)
936:             .unwrap_or(false);
937: 
938:         if !success {
939:             let error = value
940:                 .get("error")
941:                 .and_then(Value::as_str)
942:                 .unwrap_or("request failed");
943:             if command != "set_auto_compaction" {
944:                 self.agent.note(format!("{command}: {error}"));
945:             }
946:             if command == "prompt" || command == "abort" {
947:                 self.agent.busy = false;
948:             }
949:             return;
950:         }
951: 
952:         match command {
953:             "get_state" => {
954:                 if let Some(model) = value.pointer("/data/model") {
955:                     self.model = model
956:                         .get("name")
957:                         .or_else(|| model.get("id"))
958:                         .and_then(Value::as_str)
959:                         .map(str::to_string);
960:                 }
961:                 if let Some(streaming) = value.pointer("/data/isStreaming").and_then(Value::as_bool)
962:                 {
963:                     self.agent.busy = streaming;
964:                 }
965:             }
966:             "get_commands" => {
967:                 self.commands.clear();
968:                 if let Some(commands) = value.pointer("/data/commands").and_then(Value::as_array) {
969:                     for command in commands {
970:                         if let Some(name) = command.get("name").and_then(Value::as_str) {
971:                             self.commands.push(PiCommand {
972:                                 name: name.to_string(),
973:                                 description: command
974:                                     .get("description")
975:                                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
976:                                     .unwrap_or_default()
977:                                     .to_string(),
978:                             });
979:                         }
980:                     }
981:                 }
982:             }
983:             "get_available_models" => {
984:                 self.models = value
985:                     .pointer("/data/models")
986:                     .and_then(Value::as_array)
987:                     .cloned()
988:                     .unwrap_or_default();
989:             }
990:             "set_model" => {
991:                 let name = value
992:                     .pointer("/data/name")
993:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
994:                     .or_else(|| value.pointer("/data/id").and_then(Value::as_str));
995:                 if let Some(name) = name {
996:                     self.model = Some(name.to_string());
997:                     self.set_status(format!("model {name}"));
998:                 } else {
999:                     self.set_status("model changed");
1000:                 }
1001:             }
1002:             "compact" => {
1003:                 self.agent.note("context compacted");
1004:                 self.request_context_stats(false);
1005:             }
1006:             "new_session" => {
1007:                 self.agent.reset();
1008:                 self.context_percent = None;
1009:                 self.auto_compacted = false;
1010:                 self.set_status("new session");
1011:             }
1012:             "set_thinking_level" => {
1013:                 if let Some(level) = value.pointer("/data/level").and_then(Value::as_str) {
1014:                     self.agent.note(format!("thinking {level}"));
1015:                 }
1016:             }
1017:             "get_session_stats" => {
1018:                 let percent = value
1019:                     .pointer("/data/contextUsage/percent")
1020:                     .and_then(Value::as_u64);
1021:                 self.context_percent = percent;
1022:                 if std::mem::take(&mut self.stats_notice) {
1023:                     let messages = value
1024:                         .pointer("/data/totalMessages")
1025:                         .and_then(Value::as_u64)
1026:                         .unwrap_or(0);
1027:                     let tokens = value
1028:                         .pointer("/data/tokens/total")
1029:                         .and_then(Value::as_u64)
1030:                         .unwrap_or(0);
1031:                     self.agent.note(format!(
1032:                         "session  {messages} messages  {tokens} tokens  {}% context",
1033:                         percent.unwrap_or(0)
1034:                     ));
1035:                 }
1036:                 if let Some(percent) = percent {
1037:                     self.maybe_auto_compact(percent);
1038:                 }
1039:             }
1040:             "set_session_name" => self.set_status("session named"),
1041:             "export_html" => {
1042:                 if let Some(path) = value.pointer("/data/path").and_then(Value::as_str) {
1043:                     self.agent.note(format!("exported {path}"));
1044:                 }
1045:             }
1046:             "prompt" => {
1047:                 let disposition = value
1048:                     .pointer("/data/disposition")
1049:                     .and_then(Value::as_str)
1050:                     .unwrap_or("started");
1051:                 if disposition == "handled" {
1052:                     self.agent.busy = false;
1053:                 }
1054:             }
1055:             "abort" => {
1056:                 self.agent.busy = false;
1057:                 self.set_status("agent stopped");
1058:             }
1059:             _ => {}
1060:         }
1061:     }
1062: 
1063:     fn on_tool_start(&mut self, value: &Value) {
1064:         let id = value
1065:             .get("toolCallId")
1066:             .and_then(Value::as_str)
1067:             .unwrap_or_default()
1068:             .to_string();
1069:         let name = value
1070:             .get("toolName")
1071:             .and_then(Value::as_str)
1072:             .unwrap_or("tool");
1073:         let args = value.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null);
1074:         let path = tool_path(&args).map(|raw| self.resolve(&raw));
1075:         let kind = match name {
1076:             "read" => Some(TrackKind::Read),
1077:             "write" | "edit" => Some(TrackKind::Write),
1078:             _ => None,
1079:         };
1080:         let label = tool_label(name, &args, path.as_deref(), &self.root);
1081:         let body = tool_body(name, &args);
1082:         self.agent.push_tool(&id, label, ToolState::Running, body);
1083: 
1084:         let Some(path) = path else {
1085:             return;
1086:         };
1087: 
1088:         let before = fs::read_to_string(&path).ok();
1089:         let (start, end) = if kind == Some(TrackKind::Read) {
1090:             read_range(&args, before.as_deref())
1091:         } else {
1092:             let total = before
1093:                 .as_deref()
1094:                 .map(|content| content.lines().count())
1095:                 .unwrap_or(0)
1096:                 .max(1);
1097:             (1, total)
1098:         };
1099: 
1100:         if let Some(kind) = kind {
1101:             self.set_track_start(path.clone(), kind, start, end);
1102:             if path.is_file() {
1103:                 self.follow_file(&path);
1104:             }
1105:         }
1106: 
1107:         self.pending.insert(
1108:             id,
1109:             PendingTool {
1110:                 kind,
1111:                 path: Some(path),
1112:                 before,
1113:                 start,
1114:                 end,
1115:             },
1116:         );
1117:     }
1118: 
1119:     fn on_tool_end(&mut self, value: &Value) {
1120:         let id = value
1121:             .get("toolCallId")
1122:             .and_then(Value::as_str)
1123:             .unwrap_or_default()
1124:             .to_string();
1125:         let failed = value
1126:             .get("isError")
1127:             .and_then(Value::as_bool)
1128:             .unwrap_or(false);
1129:         self.agent.update_tool(
1130:             &id,
1131:             if failed {
1132:                 ToolState::Failed
1133:             } else {
1134:                 ToolState::Done
1135:             },
1136:         );
1137: 
1138:         let Some(pending) = self.pending.remove(&id) else {
1139:             return;
1140:         };
1141:         let Some(path) = pending.path else {
1142:             return;
1143:         };
1144: 
1145:         if let Some(kind) = pending.kind {
1146:             let after = fs::read_to_string(&path).ok();
1147:             let (start, end) = if kind == TrackKind::Read {
1148:                 (pending.start, pending.end)
1149:             } else {
1150:                 changed_range(pending.before.as_deref(), after.as_deref())
1151:                     .unwrap_or((pending.start, pending.end))
1152:             };
1153:             self.set_track_end(path.clone(), kind, start, end);
1154: 
1155:             if kind == TrackKind::Write {
1156:                 self.refresh_tree();
1157:                 if !failed {
1158:                     self.follow_file(&path);
1159:                     self.reload_after_tool(&path);
1160:                 }
1161:             }
1162:         }
1163:     }
1164: 
1165:     fn follow_file(&mut self, path: &Path) {
1166:         if path.strip_prefix(&self.root).is_err() {
1167:             return;
1168:         }
1169:         if let Some(buffer) = self.editor.buffer() {
1170:             if buffer.dirty && buffer.path != path {
1171:                 return;
1172:             }
1173:         }
1174:         let _ = self.editor.open(path);
1175:         self.tree.reveal(path);
1176:     }
1177: 
1178:     fn reload_after_tool(&mut self, path: &Path) {
1179:         let Some(index) = self.editor.find_buffer(path) else {
1180:             return;
1181:         };
1182:         if self.editor.buffers[index].dirty {
1183:             self.editor.buffers[index].external_change = true;
1184:             self.set_status(format!("{} changed on disk", self.rel(path)));
1185:         } else if let Err(error) = self.editor.reload_at(index) {
1186:             self.set_status(error);
1187:         }
1188:     }
1189: 
1190:     fn resolve(&self, raw: &str) -> PathBuf {
1191:         let path = PathBuf::from(raw);
1192:         if path.is_absolute() {
1193:             path
1194:         } else {
1195:             self.root.join(path)
1196:         }
1197:     }
1198: 
1199:     pub fn rel(&self, path: &Path) -> String {
1200:         self.editor.rel_of(path)
1201:     }
1202: 
1203:     fn set_track_start(&mut self, path: PathBuf, kind: TrackKind, start: usize, end: usize) {
1204:         self.tracks
1205:             .retain(|track| track.path != path || track.active);
1206:         self.tracks.push(Track {
1207:             path,
1208:             start,
1209:             end,
1210:             kind,
1211:             active: true,
1212:             until: self.now + Duration::from_secs(120),
1213:         });
1214:         if self.tracks.len() > 6 {
1215:             self.tracks.remove(0);
1216:         }
1217:     }
1218: 
1219:     fn set_track_end(&mut self, path: PathBuf, kind: TrackKind, start: usize, end: usize) {
1220:         let until = self.now + Duration::from_secs(3);
1221:         if let Some(track) = self
1222:             .tracks
1223:             .iter_mut()
1224:             .rev()
1225:             .find(|track| track.path == path && track.kind == kind)
1226:         {
1227:             track.start = start;
1228:             track.end = end;
1229:             track.active = false;
1230:             track.until = until;
1231:         } else {
1232:             self.tracks.push(Track {
1233:                 path,
1234:                 start,
1235:                 end,
1236:                 kind,
1237:                 active: false,
1238:                 until,
1239:             });
1240:         }
1241:     }
1242: 
1243:     pub fn track_label_for(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
1244:         let track = self
1245:             .tracks
1246:             .iter()
1247:             .rev()
1248:             .find(|track| track.path == path && (track.active || track.until > self.now))?;
1249:         let kind = match track.kind {
1250:             TrackKind::Read => "read",
1251:             TrackKind::Write => "write",
1252:         };
1253:         Some(format!("{kind} {}-{}", track.start, track.end))
1254:     }
1255: 
1256:     pub fn tick(&mut self) {
1257:         self.now = Instant::now();
1258: 
1259:         if self.last_tree_refresh.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(2) {
1260:             self.refresh_tree();
1261:         }
1262: 
1263:         let clear_status = self
1264:             .status
1265:             .as_ref()
1266:             .map(|(_, at)| self.now.duration_since(*at) > Duration::from_secs(5))
1267:             .unwrap_or(false);
1268:         if clear_status {
1269:             self.status = None;
1270:         }
1271: 
1272:         for track in &mut self.tracks {
1273:             if track.active && self.now >= track.until {
1274:                 track.active = false;
1275:             }
1276:         }
1277:         self.tracks
1278:             .retain(|track| track.active || track.until > self.now);
1279: 
1280:         let mut project_lines = None;
1281:         let mut project_lines_done = false;
1282:         if let Some(rx) = &self.project_lines {
1283:             match rx.try_recv() {
1284:                 Ok(lines) => project_lines = Some(lines),
1285:                 Err(TryRecvError::Empty) => {}
1286:                 Err(TryRecvError::Disconnected) => project_lines_done = true,
1287:             }
1288:         }
1289:         if project_lines_done {
1290:             self.project_lines = None;
1291:         }
1292:         if let Some(lines) = project_lines {
1293:             self.project_lines = None;
1294:             self.editor.add_project_lines(lines);
1295:         }
1296: 
1297:         self.editor.tick(self.now);
1298: 
1299:         if let Some(result) = self.git.poll_push() {
1300:             match result {
1301:                 Ok(message) => {
1302:                     self.set_status(message);
1303:                     self.git.refresh();
1304:                 }
1305:                 Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
1306:             }
1307:         }
1308:         if self.git.repo
1309:             && !self.git.pushing
1310:             && self.git.last_refresh.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(2)
1311:         {
1312:             self.git.refresh();
1313:         }
1314:     }
1315: 
1316:     fn refresh_tree(&mut self) {
1317:         self.tree.refresh();
1318:         self.project_files = self.tree.files();
1319:         let removed = self.editor.close_missing();
1320:         if !removed.is_empty() {
1321:             self.set_status(if removed.len() == 1 {
1322:                 format!("closed deleted {}", removed[0])
1323:             } else {
1324:                 format!("closed {} deleted files", removed.len())
1325:             });
1326:         }
1327:         if !self.agent.suggestions.is_empty() {
1328:             self.agent.update_suggestions(&self.project_files);
1329:         }
1330:         self.last_tree_refresh = Instant::now();
1331:     }
1332: 
1333:     fn refresh_agent_suggestions(&mut self) {
1334:         self.agent.suggestion_selected = 0;
1335:         self.agent.update_suggestions(&self.project_files);
1336:     }
1337: 
1338:     pub fn send_prompt(&mut self) {
1339:         let text = self.agent.input.trim().to_string();
1340:         if text.is_empty() {
1341:             return;
1342:         }
1343:         if text.starts_with('/') {
1344:             self.handle_agent_command(&text);
1345:             return;
1346:         }
1347:         if matches!(self.pi, PiState::Missing) {
1348:             self.set_status("pi is not installed");
1349:             return;
1350:         }
1351:         if let PiState::Failed(error) = &self.pi {
1352:             self.set_status(error.clone());
1353:             return;
1354:         }
1355: 
1356:         self.agent.push_user(&text);
1357:         self.agent.clear_input();
1358:         self.send_text_prompt(&text);
1359:         self.agent.scroll = 0;
1360:     }
1361: 
1362:     fn send_text_prompt(&mut self, text: &str) {
1363:         let busy = self.agent.busy;
1364:         if let PiState::Running(client) = &mut self.pi {
1365:             match client.prompt(text, busy) {
1366:                 Ok(()) => self.agent.busy = true,
1367:                 Err(error) => {
1368:                     self.agent.note(format!("prompt failed: {error}"));
1369:                     self.agent.busy = false;
1370:                 }
1371:             }
1372:         }
1373:         self.agent.scroll = 0;
1374:     }
1375: 
1376:     fn request_context_stats(&mut self, announce: bool) {
1377:         if !matches!(self.pi, PiState::Running(_)) {
1378:             return;
1379:         }
1380:         if announce {
1381:             self.stats_notice = true;
1382:         }
1383:         self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "get_session_stats" }));
1384:     }
1385: 
1386:     fn maybe_auto_compact(&mut self, percent: u64) {
1387:         if percent < AUTO_COMPACT_PERCENT {
1388:             self.auto_compacted = false;
1389:             return;
1390:         }
1391:         if !should_auto_compact(percent, self.auto_compacted)
1392:             || self.agent.busy
1393:             || !matches!(self.pi, PiState::Running(_))
1394:         {
1395:             return;
1396:         }
1397:         self.auto_compacted = true;
1398:         self.set_status(format!("context at {percent}%, compacting"));
1399:         self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "compact" }));
1400:     }
1401: 
1402:     fn send_rpc(&mut self, command: Value) {
1403:         match &mut self.pi {
1404:             PiState::Running(client) => {
1405:                 if let Err(error) = client.send(command) {
1406:                     self.agent.note(format!("pi command failed: {error}"));
1407:                 }
1408:             }
1409:             PiState::Missing => self.agent.note("pi is not installed"),
1410:             PiState::Failed(error) => self.agent.note(error.clone()),
1411:         }
1412:     }
1413: 
1414:     fn handle_agent_command(&mut self, text: &str) {
1415:         let Some(body) = text.strip_prefix('/') else {
1416:             return;
1417:         };
1418:         self.agent.push_user(text);
1419:         self.agent.clear_input();
1420:         self.agent.scroll = 0;
1421: 
1422:         let mut parts = body.splitn(2, char::is_whitespace);
1423:         let name = parts.next().unwrap_or_default();
1424:         let args = parts.next().unwrap_or_default().trim();
1425: 
1426:         match name {
1427:             "help" => {
1428:                 let help = self.command_help();
1429:                 self.agent.note(help);
1430:             }
1431:             "new" | "reset" => self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "new_session" })),
1432:             "compact" => {
1433:                 let mut command = json!({ "type": "compact" });
1434:                 if !args.is_empty() {
1435:                     command["customInstructions"] = json!(args);
1436:                 }
1437:                 self.send_rpc(command);
1438:             }
1439:             "abort" | "stop" => self.abort_agent(),
1440:             "model" => self.model_command(args),
1441:             "thinking" => self.thinking_command(args),
1442:             "stats" | "session" => self.request_context_stats(true),
1443:             "name" => {
1444:                 if args.is_empty() {
1445:                     self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "get_state" }));
1446:                 } else {
1447:                     self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "set_session_name", "name": args }));
1448:                 }
1449:             }
1450:             "export" => {
1451:                 let mut command = json!({ "type": "export_html" });
1452:                 if !args.is_empty() {
1453:                     command["outputPath"] = json!(args);
1454:                 }
1455:                 self.send_rpc(command);
1456:             }
1457:             "copy" => match self.agent.last_assistant() {
1458:                 Some(text) => {
1459:                     self.editor.clipboard = text.clone();
1460:                     match copy_to_clipboard(&text) {
1461:                         Ok(()) => self.set_status("copied"),
1462:                         Err(error) => self.agent.note(error),
1463:                     }
1464:                 }
1465:                 None => self.agent.note("nothing to copy"),
1466:             },
1467:             "hide" => self.set_agent_hidden(true),
1468:             "show" => self.set_agent_hidden(false),
1469:             "login" | "logout" => self.agent.note(format!(
1470:                 "/{name} runs inside pi's terminal, then restart smol"
1471:             )),
1472:             _ => {
1473:                 if self.commands.iter().any(|command| command.name == name) {
1474:                     self.send_text_prompt(text);
1475:                 } else if is_pi_terminal_command(name) {
1476:                     self.agent
1477:                         .note(format!("/{name} runs inside pi's terminal"));
1478:                 } else {
1479:                     self.agent
1480:                         .note(format!("unknown command /{name}, type /help"));
1481:                 }
1482:             }
1483:         }
1484:     }
1485: 
1486:     fn model_command(&mut self, args: &str) {
1487:         if args.is_empty() {
1488:             self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "get_available_models" }));
1489:             return;
1490:         }
1491:         if let Some((provider, id)) = args.split_once('/') {
1492:             self.send_rpc(json!({
1493:                 "type": "set_model",
1494:                 "provider": provider,
1495:                 "modelId": id
1496:             }));
1497:             return;
1498:         }
1499: 
1500:         let found = self.models.iter().find_map(|model| {
1501:             let id = model.get("id").and_then(Value::as_str)?;
1502:             let name = model
1503:                 .get("name")
1504:                 .and_then(Value::as_str)
1505:                 .unwrap_or_default();
1506:             let provider = model.get("provider").and_then(Value::as_str)?;
1507:             let matches =
1508:                 id.eq_ignore_ascii_case(args) || name.to_lowercase().contains(&args.to_lowercase());
1509:             matches.then(|| (provider.to_string(), id.to_string()))
1510:         });
1511: 
1512:         if let Some((provider, id)) = found {
1513:             self.send_rpc(json!({
1514:                 "type": "set_model",
1515:                 "provider": provider,
1516:                 "modelId": id
1517:             }));
1518:         } else {
1519:             self.agent
1520:                 .note("use /model provider/model, or /model to list models");
1521:         }
1522:     }
1523: 
1524:     fn thinking_command(&mut self, args: &str) {
1525:         const LEVELS: [&str; 7] = ["off", "minimal", "low", "medium", "high", "xhigh", "max"];
1526:         if args.is_empty() {
1527:             self.agent
1528:                 .note(format!("thinking levels  {}", LEVELS.join("  ")));
1529:             return;
1530:         }
1531:         if LEVELS.contains(&args) {
1532:             self.send_rpc(json!({ "type": "set_thinking_level", "level": args }));
1533:         } else {
1534:             self.agent
1535:                 .note("thinking levels  off minimal low medium high xhigh max");
1536:         }
1537:     }
1538: 
1539:     fn command_help(&self) -> String {
1540:         let mut text = String::from(
1541:             "commands\n/help  /new  /compact [instructions]  /model [provider/model]  /thinking [level]\n/abort  /stats  /name [name]  /export [path]  /copy  /hide  /show\n",
1542:         );
1543:         if !self.commands.is_empty() {
1544:             text.push_str("\npi resources\n");
1545:             for command in self.commands.iter().take(16) {
1546:                 let description = truncate(&command.description, 72);
1547:                 if description.is_empty() {
1548:                     text.push_str(&format!("/{}\n", command.name));
1549:                 } else {
1550:                     text.push_str(&format!("/{}  {}\n", command.name, description));
1551:                 }
1552:             }
1553:         }
1554:         text.push_str(
1555:             "\n/settings /resume /tree /fork /clone /share /login run inside pi's terminal",
1556:         );
1557:         text
1558:     }
1559: 
1560:     pub fn abort_agent(&mut self) {
1561:         if let PiState::Running(client) = &mut self.pi {
1562:             if client.abort().is_ok() {
1563:                 self.set_status("stopping agent");
1564:             }
1565:         }
1566:     }
1567: 
1568:     pub fn handle_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent) {
1569:         if key.kind == KeyEventKind::Release {
1570:             return;
1571:         }
1572:         let ctrl = key.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
1573:         let alt = key.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::ALT);
1574: 
1575:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('q') {
1576:             self.should_quit = true;
1577:             return;
1578:         }
1579:         if self.dialog.is_some() {
1580:             self.handle_dialog_key(key, ctrl, alt);
1581:             return;
1582:         }
1583:         if self.context_menu.is_some() {
1584:             self.handle_context_key(key);
1585:             return;
1586:         }
1587:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('p') {
1588:             self.toggle_menu();
1589:             return;
1590:         }
1591:         if self.menu.is_some() {
1592:             self.handle_menu_key(key);
1593:             return;
1594:         }
1595:         if self.diff.is_some() {
1596:             if matches!(key.code, KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Char('q')) {
1597:                 self.diff = None;
1598:                 return;
1599:             }
1600:             if let Some(diff) = &mut self.diff {
1601:                 match key.code {
1602:                     KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_sub(1),
1603:                     KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => {
1604:                         diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_add(1)
1605:                     }
1606:                     KeyCode::PageUp => diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_sub(10),
1607:                     KeyCode::PageDown => diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_add(10),
1608:                     KeyCode::Home => diff.scroll = 0,
1609:                     KeyCode::End => diff.scroll = diff.lines.len().saturating_sub(1),
1610:                     _ => {}
1611:                 }
1612:             }
1613:             return;
1614:         }
1615:         if key.code == KeyCode::F(6) {
1616:             self.cycle_focus();
1617:             return;
1618:         }
1619:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('g') {
1620:             self.sidebar = match self.sidebar {
1621:                 Sidebar::Files => Sidebar::Git,
1622:                 Sidebar::Git => Sidebar::Files,
1623:             };
1624:             self.focus = Focus::Tree;
1625:             self.git.refresh();
1626:             return;
1627:         }
1628:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('b') {
1629:             self.toggle_agent();
1630:             return;
1631:         }
1632:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('s') {
1633:             self.save();
1634:             return;
1635:         }
1636:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('c') {
1637:             if self.focus == Focus::Editor {
1638:                 if let Some(text) = self.editor.selection_text() {
1639:                     if !text.is_empty() {
1640:                         self.copy_text(&text, "copied");
1641:                     }
1642:                 }
1643:             }
1644:             return;
1645:         }
1646:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('x') {
1647:             if self.focus == Focus::Editor {
1648:                 if let Some(text) = self.editor.cut_selection() {
1649:                     self.copy_text(&text, "cut");
1650:                 }
1651:             }
1652:             return;
1653:         }
1654:         if ctrl && key.code == KeyCode::Char('v') {
1655:             match self.focus {
1656:                 Focus::Editor => self.paste_editor(),
1657:                 Focus::Agent => self.paste_agent(),
1658:                 Focus::Tree => {}
1659:             }
1660:             return;
1661:         }
1662:         if alt {
1663:             match key.code {
1664:                 KeyCode::Char('1') => {
1665:                     self.focus = Focus::Tree;
1666:                     return;
1667:                 }
1668:                 KeyCode::Char('2') => {
1669:                     self.focus = Focus::Editor;
1670:                     return;
1671:                 }
1672:                 KeyCode::Char('3') => {
1673:                     self.agent_hidden = false;
1674:                     self.focus = Focus::Agent;
1675:                     return;
1676:                 }
1677:                 _ => {}
1678:             }
1679:         }
1680: 
1681:         match self.focus {
1682:             Focus::Tree => self.handle_sidebar_key(key, ctrl, alt),
1683:             Focus::Editor => self.handle_editor_key(key, ctrl, alt),
1684:             Focus::Agent => self.handle_agent_key(key, ctrl, alt),
1685:         }
1686:     }
1687: 
1688:     fn handle_sidebar_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent, ctrl: bool, alt: bool) {
1689:         match self.sidebar {
1690:             Sidebar::Files => self.handle_tree_key(key),
1691:             Sidebar::Git => self.handle_git_key(key, ctrl, alt),
1692:         }
1693:     }
1694: 
1695:     fn handle_git_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent, ctrl: bool, alt: bool) {
1696:         if self.git.input_focus {
1697:             match key.code {
1698:                 KeyCode::Esc => {
1699:                     self.git.input_focus = false;
1700:                     self.git.confirm_discard = false;
1701:                 }
1702:                 KeyCode::Enter => self.commit_git(),
1703:                 KeyCode::Backspace => self.git.backspace_input(),
1704:                 KeyCode::Delete => self.git.delete_input(),
1705:                 KeyCode::Left => self.git.move_input(-1),
1706:                 KeyCode::Right => self.git.move_input(1),
1707:                 KeyCode::Home => self.git.input_home(),
1708:                 KeyCode::End => self.git.input_end(),
1709:                 KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => self.git.clear_input(),
1710:                 KeyCode::Char(ch) if !ctrl && !alt => self.git.insert_input(ch),
1711:                 _ => {}
1712:             }
1713:             return;
1714:         }
1715: 
1716:         if self.git.confirm_discard && !matches!(key.code, KeyCode::Char('d')) {
1717:             self.git.confirm_discard = false;
1718:         }
1719: 
1720:         match key.code {
1721:             KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => self.git.move_selection(-1),
1722:             KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => self.git.move_selection(1),
1723:             KeyCode::Home => self.git.selected = 0,
1724:             KeyCode::End => {
1725:                 self.git.selected = self.git.changes.len().saturating_sub(1);
1726:             }
1727:             KeyCode::PageUp => self.git.move_selection(-10),
1728:             KeyCode::PageDown => self.git.move_selection(10),
1729:             KeyCode::Enter => self.open_diff(),
1730:             KeyCode::Char('o') => {
1731:                 if let Some(path) = self.git.selected_path() {
1732:                     self.open_file(&path);
1733:                 }
1734:             }
1735:             KeyCode::Char('c') => self.git.input_focus = true,
1736:             KeyCode::Char('p') => self.push_git(),
1737:             KeyCode::Char('d') => self.discard_git(),
1738:             KeyCode::Char('r') => {
1739:                 self.git.refresh();
1740:                 self.set_status("git status refreshed");
1741:             }
1742:             _ => {}
1743:         }
1744:     }
1745: 
1746:     fn commit_git(&mut self) {
1747:         if self.git.input.trim().is_empty() {
1748:             self.set_status("commit message is empty");
1749:             return;
1750:         }
1751:         match self.git.commit() {
1752:             Ok(message) => self.set_status(message),
1753:             Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
1754:         }
1755:     }

1761:                     .lines()

1770:                     lines,
1771:                     scroll: 0,
1772:                 });
1773:                 self.unsupported = None;
1774:             }
1775:             Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
1776:         }
1777:     }

1923:     fn handle_menu_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent) {
1924:         let Some(menu) = self.menu.as_mut() else {
1925:             return;
1926:         };
1927:         let Some(page) = menu.pages.last_mut() else {
1928:             self.menu = None;
1929:             return;
1930:         };
1931:         match key.code {
1932:             KeyCode::Esc => {
1933:                 let close = menu.pages.len() <= 1;
1934:                 menu.pages.pop();
1935:                 if close {
1936:                     self.menu = None;
1937:                 }
1938:             }
1939:             KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => page.selected = page.selected.saturating_sub(1),
1940:             KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => {
1941:                 page.selected = (page.selected + 1).min(page.items.len().saturating_sub(1))
1942:             }
1943:             KeyCode::Home => page.selected = 0,
1944:             KeyCode::End => page.selected = page.items.len().saturating_sub(1),
1945:             KeyCode::Enter => {
1946:                 let action = page.items.get(page.selected).map(|item| item.action);
1947:                 if let Some(action) = action {
1948:                     self.run_menu_command(action);
1949:                 }
1950:             }
1951:             _ => {}
1952:         }
1953:     }
1954: 
1955:     fn run_menu_command(&mut self, action: MenuAction) {
1956:         if let MenuAction::OpenMenu(kind) = action {
1957:             let page = self.menu_page(kind);
1958:             if let Some(menu) = self.menu.as_mut() {
1959:                 menu.pages.push(page);
1960:             }
1961:             return;
1962:         }
1963:         let keep_open = matches!(
1964:             action,
1965:             MenuAction::ToggleWrap | MenuAction::ToggleAgent | MenuAction::ToggleGit
1966:         );
1967:         if !keep_open {
1968:             self.menu = None;
1969:         }
1970:         match action {
1971:             MenuAction::OpenMenu(_) => {}
1972:             MenuAction::ToggleWrap => {
1973:                 let wrap = self.editor.toggle_wrap();
1974:                 self.set_status(if wrap {
1975:                     "word wrap on"
1976:                 } else {
1977:                     "word wrap off"
1978:                 });
1979:             }
1980:             MenuAction::ToggleAgent => self.toggle_agent(),
1981:             MenuAction::ToggleGit => {
1982:                 self.sidebar = match self.sidebar {
1983:                     Sidebar::Files => {
1984:                         self.git.refresh();
1985:                         Sidebar::Git
1986:                     }
1987:                     Sidebar::Git => Sidebar::Files,
1988:                 };
1989:                 self.focus = Focus::Tree;
1990:             }
1991:             MenuAction::Save => self.save(),
1992:             MenuAction::CloseFile => match self.editor.close_active() {
1993:                 Ok(message) => self.set_status(message),
1994:                 Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
1995:             },
1996:             MenuAction::NextFile => self.editor.switch_buffer(1),
1997:             MenuAction::PreviousFile => self.editor.switch_buffer(-1),
1998:             MenuAction::RefreshGit => {
1999:                 self.git.refresh();
2000:                 self.set_status("git status refreshed");

38: pub struct Buffer {
39:     pub path: PathBuf,
40:     pub rel: String,
41:     pub lines: Vec<String>,
42:     pub cursor_line: usize,
43:     pub cursor_col: usize,
44:     pub scroll: usize,
45:     pub hscroll: usize,
46:     pub selection: Option<(usize, usize)>,
47:     pub dirty: bool,
48:     pub external_change: bool,

55: impl Buffer {
56:     fn remember(&mut self, coalesce: bool) {
57:         let now = Instant::now();
58:         let new_group = self.undo_group.map_or(true, |last| {
59:             now.duration_since(last) > Duration::from_millis(700)
60:         });
61:         if !coalesce || new_group {
62:             self.undo.push(self.snapshot());
63:             if self.undo.len() > 200 {
64:                 self.undo.remove(0);
65:             }
66:         }
67:         self.undo_group = Some(now);
68:     }
69: 
70:     fn snapshot(&self) -> Snapshot {
71:         Snapshot {
72:             lines: self.lines.clone(),
73:             cursor_line: self.cursor_line,
74:             cursor_col: self.cursor_col,
75:             selection: self.selection,
76:         }
77:     }
78: 
79:     pub fn matching_spans(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&[(Style, String)]> {
80:         let line = self.lines.get(index)?;
81:         let spans = self.spans.get(index)?;
82:         spans_match_line(line, spans).then_some(spans.as_slice())
83:     }
84: 
85:     fn restore(&mut self, snapshot: Snapshot) {
86:         self.lines = snapshot.lines;
87:         self.cursor_line = snapshot.cursor_line.min(self.lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
88:         self.cursor_col = snapshot
89:             .cursor_col
90:             .min(char_count(&self.lines[self.cursor_line]));
91:         self.selection = snapshot.selection;
92:         self.dirty = true;
93:         self.highlight_dirty = true;
94:         self.undo_group = None;
95:     }
96: }

100:     pub buffers: Vec<Buffer>,
101:     pub active: usize,
102:     pub wrap: bool,
103:     pub ghost: Option<String>,
104:     pub clipboard: String,
105:     predictor: Predictor,
106:     highlighter: Arc<Highlighter>,
107:     highlight_tx: Sender<HighlightResult>,
108:     highlight_rx: Receiver<HighlightResult>,
