115: impl Editor {
116:     pub fn new(root: &Path) -> Self {
117:         let root = fs::canonicalize(root).unwrap_or_else(|_| root.to_path_buf());
118:         let (highlight_tx, highlight_rx) = mpsc::channel();
119:         Self {
120:             root,
121:             buffers: Vec::new(),
122:             active: 0,
123:             wrap: true,
124:             ghost: None,
125:             clipboard: String::new(),
126:             predictor: Predictor::load(),
127:             highlighter: Arc::new(Highlighter::new()),
128:             highlight_tx,
129:             highlight_rx,
130:             highlight_inflight: 0,
131:             last_edit: Instant::now(),
132:             version: 0,
133:             prediction_key: None,
134:         }
135:     }
136: 
137:     pub fn add_project_lines(&mut self, lines: Vec<String>) {
138:         self.predictor.add_project_lines(lines);
139:         self.touch();
140:     }
141: 
142:     pub fn buffer(&self) -> Option<&Buffer> {
143:         self.buffers.get(self.active)
144:     }
145: 
146:     pub fn find_buffer(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<usize> {
147:         self.buffers.iter().position(|buffer| buffer.path == path)
148:     }
149: 
150:     pub fn rel_of(&self, path: &Path) -> String {
151:         let relative = path
152:             .strip_prefix(&self.root)
153:             .map(|value| value.to_string_lossy().to_string())
154:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| path.display().to_string());
155:         if relative.is_empty() {
156:             path.file_name()
157:                 .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().to_string())
158:                 .unwrap_or(relative)
159:         } else {
160:             relative
161:         }
162:     }
163: 
164:     pub fn rename_path(&mut self, old: &Path, new: &Path) {
165:         let old = fs::canonicalize(old).unwrap_or_else(|_| old.to_path_buf());
166:         let root = self.root.clone();
167:         let mut changed = false;
168:         for buffer in &mut self.buffers {
169:             let Ok(suffix) = buffer.path.strip_prefix(&old) else {
170:                 continue;
171:             };
172:             let path = if suffix.as_os_str().is_empty() {
173:                 new.to_path_buf()
174:             } else {
175:                 new.join(suffix)
176:             };
177:             buffer.rel = relative_label(&root, &path);
178:             buffer.path = path;
179:             buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
180:             changed = true;
181:         }
182:         if changed {
183:             self.version += 1;
184:             self.clear_prediction();
185:         }
186:     }
187: 
188:     pub fn remove_paths(&mut self, target: &Path) -> bool {
189:         let target = fs::canonicalize(target).unwrap_or_else(|_| target.to_path_buf());
190:         let before = self.buffers.len();
191:         self.buffers
192:             .retain(|buffer| buffer.path != target && !buffer.path.starts_with(&target));
193:         if self.buffers.len() == before {
194:             return false;
195:         }
196:         self.active = self.active.min(self.buffers.len().saturating_sub(1));
197:         self.version += 1;
198:         self.clear_prediction();
199:         true
200:     }
201: 
202:     pub fn close_missing(&mut self) -> Vec<String> {
203:         let root = self.root.clone();
204:         let mut removed = Vec::new();
205:         self.buffers.retain(|buffer| {
206:             if buffer.path.exists() {
207:                 true
208:             } else {
209:                 removed.push(relative_label(&root, &buffer.path));
210:                 false
211:             }
212:         });
213:         if removed.is_empty() {
214:             return removed;
215:         }
216:         self.active = self.active.min(self.buffers.len().saturating_sub(1));
217:         self.version += 1;
218:         self.clear_prediction();
219:         removed
220:     }
221: 
222:     pub fn open(&mut self, path: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
223:         let path = fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
224:         if let Some(index) = self.find_buffer(&path) {
225:             self.active = index;
226:             if !self.buffers[index].dirty {
227:                 self.reload_at(index)?;
228:             }
229:             self.clear_prediction();
230:             return Ok(());
231:         }
232: 
233:         let lines = read_text(&path)?;
234:         let spans = self.highlighter.highlight(&path, &lines);
235:         let rel = self.rel_of(&path);
236:         self.buffers.push(Buffer {
237:             path,
238:             rel,
239:             lines,
240:             cursor_line: 0,
241:             cursor_col: 0,
242:             scroll: 0,
243:             hscroll: 0,
244:             selection: None,
245:             dirty: false,
246:             external_change: false,
247:             highlight_dirty: false,
248:             spans,
249:             undo: Vec::new(),
250:             undo_group: None,
251:         });
252:         self.active = self.buffers.len() - 1;
253:         self.clear_prediction();
254:         Ok(())
255:     }
256: 
257:     pub fn reload_at(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<(), String> {
258:         let path = self.buffers.get(index).ok_or("no buffer")?.path.clone();
259:         let lines = read_text(&path)?;
260:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[index];
261:         buffer.lines = lines;
262:         buffer.cursor_line = buffer.cursor_line.min(buffer.lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
263:         buffer.cursor_col = buffer
264:             .cursor_col
265:             .min(char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]));
266:         buffer.scroll = buffer.scroll.min(buffer.lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
267:         buffer.dirty = false;
268:         buffer.external_change = false;
269:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
270:         buffer.undo.clear();
271:         buffer.undo_group = None;
272:         self.version += 1;
273:         self.clear_prediction();
274:         Ok(())
275:     }
276: 
277:     pub fn has_selection(&self) -> bool {
278:         self.selection_range().is_some()
279:     }
280: 
281:     pub fn selection_text(&self) -> Option<String> {
282:         let ((start_line, start_col), (end_line, end_col)) = self.selection_range()?;
283:         let buffer = self.buffer()?;
284:         let mut text = String::new();
285:         for line_index in start_line..=end_line {
286:             let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[line_index].chars().collect();
287:             let start = if line_index == start_line {
288:                 start_col
289:             } else {
290:                 0
291:             };
292:             let end = if line_index == end_line {
293:                 end_col
294:             } else {
295:                 chars.len()
296:             };
297:             let start = start.min(chars.len());
298:             let end = end.min(chars.len()).max(start);
299:             text.extend(&chars[start..end]);
300:             if line_index < end_line {
301:                 text.push('\n');
302:             }
303:         }
304:         Some(text)
305:     }
306: 
307:     pub fn cut_selection(&mut self) -> Option<String> {
308:         let text = self.selection_text()?;
309:         if text.is_empty() {
310:             return None;
311:         }
312:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
313:             buffer.remember(false);
314:             buffer.dirty = true;
315:             buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
316:         }
317:         self.delete_selection_internal();
318:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
319:             buffer.undo_group = None;
320:         }
321:         self.version += 1;
322:         self.clear_prediction();
323:         Some(text)
324:     }
325: 
326:     pub fn begin_mouse_selection(&mut self, row: usize, cells: usize, width: usize) {
327:         self.place_cursor_from_screen(row, cells, width);
328:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
329:             buffer.selection = Some((buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col));
330:         }
331:     }
332: 
333:     pub fn extend_mouse_selection(&mut self, row: usize, cells: usize, width: usize) {
334:         let anchor = self
335:             .buffers
336:             .get(self.active)
337:             .and_then(|buffer| buffer.selection);
338:         self.place_cursor_from_screen(row, cells, width);
339:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
340:             buffer.selection = anchor.or(Some((buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col)));
341:         }
342:     }
343: 
344:     pub fn undo(&mut self) -> bool {
345:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
346:             return false;
347:         };
348:         let Some(snapshot) = buffer.undo.pop() else {
349:             return false;
350:         };
351:         buffer.restore(snapshot);
352:         self.version += 1;
353:         self.clear_prediction();
354:         true
355:     }
356: 
357:     pub fn save_active(&mut self) -> Result<String, String> {
358:         let (path, lines, rel) = {
359:             let buffer = self.buffer().ok_or("no file open")?;
360:             (
361:                 buffer.path.clone(),
362:                 buffer.lines.clone(),
363:                 buffer.rel.clone(),
364:             )
365:         };
366:         fs::write(&path, lines.join("\n")).map_err(|error| format!("save failed: {error}"))?;
367:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
368:             buffer.dirty = false;
369:         }
370:         self.predictor.learn_lines(&lines);
371:         Ok(format!("saved {rel}"))
372:     }
373: 
374:     pub fn close_active(&mut self) -> Result<String, String> {
375:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffer() else {
376:             return Err("no file open".into());
377:         };
378:         if buffer.dirty {
379:             return Err("file has unsaved changes".into());
380:         }
381:         let rel = buffer.rel.clone();
382:         self.buffers.remove(self.active);
383:         if !self.buffers.is_empty() {
384:             self.active = self.active.min(self.buffers.len() - 1);
385:         } else {
386:             self.active = 0;
387:         }
388:         self.version += 1;
389:         self.clear_prediction();
390:         Ok(format!("closed {rel}"))
391:     }
392: 
393:     pub fn insert_char(&mut self, ch: char) {
394:         if self.buffers.get(self.active).is_none() {
395:             return;
396:         }
397:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
398:             buffer.remember(true);
399:         }
400:         self.delete_selection_internal();
401:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
402:             return;
403:         };
404:         let mut chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
405:         let col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
406:         chars.insert(col, ch);
407:         buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line] = chars.into_iter().collect();
408:         buffer.cursor_col = col + 1;
409:         buffer.dirty = true;
410:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
411:         self.version += 1;
412:         self.touch();
413:     }
414: 
415:     pub fn insert_text(&mut self, text: &str) {
416:         let text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', "");
417:         for (index, part) in text.split('\n').enumerate() {
418:             if index > 0 {
419:                 self.newline();
420:             }
421:             for ch in part.chars() {
422:                 self.insert_char(ch);
423:             }
424:         }
425:     }
426: 
427:     pub fn newline(&mut self) {
428:         if self.buffers.get(self.active).is_none() {
429:             return;
430:         }
431:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
432:             buffer.remember(false);
433:         }
434:         self.delete_selection_internal();
435:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
436:             return;
437:         };
438:         let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
439:         let col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
440:         let before: String = chars[..col].iter().collect();
441:         let after: String = chars[col..].iter().collect();
442:         let indent: String = before.chars().take_while(|ch| ch.is_whitespace()).collect();
443:         let opens_block = matches!(
444:             before.trim_end().chars().last(),
445:             Some('{') | Some('(') | Some('[') | Some(':')
446:         );
447:         let extra = if opens_block && after.trim().is_empty() {
448:             "    "
449:         } else {
450:             ""
451:         };
452:         let next_line = format!("{indent}{extra}{after}");
453:         let next_col = indent.chars().count() + extra.chars().count();
454: 
455:         let line = buffer.cursor_line;
456:         buffer.lines[line] = before;
457:         buffer.lines.insert(line + 1, next_line);
458:         if line < buffer.spans.len() {
459:             buffer.spans.insert(line + 1, Vec::new());
460:         }
461:         buffer.cursor_line += 1;
462:         buffer.cursor_col = next_col;
463:         buffer.dirty = true;
464:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
465:         self.version += 1;
466:         self.touch();
467:     }
468: 
469:     pub fn backspace(&mut self) {
470:         let has_selection = self.selection_range().is_some();
471:         let can_change = has_selection
472:             || self
473:                 .buffers
474:                 .get(self.active)
475:                 .map(|buffer| buffer.cursor_col > 0 || buffer.cursor_line > 0)
476:                 .unwrap_or(false);
477:         if !can_change {
478:             return;
479:         }
480:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
481:             buffer.remember(false);
482:         }
483:         if self.delete_selection_internal() {
484:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
485:                 buffer.dirty = true;
486:                 buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
487:             }
488:             self.version += 1;
489:             self.touch();
490:             return;
491:         }
492:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
493:             return;
494:         };
495:         if buffer.cursor_col > 0 {
496:             let mut chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
497:             let col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
498:             chars.remove(col - 1);
499:             buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line] = chars.into_iter().collect();
500:             buffer.cursor_col = col - 1;
501:         } else if buffer.cursor_line > 0 {
502:             let removed = buffer.cursor_line;
503:             let current = buffer.lines.remove(removed);
504:             if removed < buffer.spans.len() {
505:                 buffer.spans.remove(removed);
506:             }
507:             let previous = &mut buffer.lines[removed - 1];
508:             let col = previous.chars().count();
509:             previous.push_str(&current);
510:             buffer.cursor_line -= 1;
511:             buffer.cursor_col = col;
512:         } else {
513:             return;
514:         }
515:         buffer.dirty = true;
516:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
517:         self.version += 1;
518:         self.touch();
519:     }
520: 
521:     pub fn delete(&mut self) {
522:         let has_selection = self.selection_range().is_some();
523:         let can_change = has_selection
524:             || self
525:                 .buffers
526:                 .get(self.active)
527:                 .map(|buffer| {
528:                     buffer.cursor_col < char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line])
529:                         || buffer.cursor_line + 1 < buffer.lines.len()
530:                 })
531:                 .unwrap_or(false);
532:         if !can_change {
533:             return;
534:         }
535:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
536:             buffer.remember(false);
537:         }
538:         if self.delete_selection_internal() {
539:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
540:                 buffer.dirty = true;
541:                 buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
542:             }
543:             self.version += 1;
544:             self.touch();
545:             return;
546:         }
547:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
548:             return;
549:         };
550:         let length = char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]);
551:         if buffer.cursor_col < length {
552:             let mut chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
553:             chars.remove(buffer.cursor_col);
554:             buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line] = chars.into_iter().collect();
555:         } else if buffer.cursor_line + 1 < buffer.lines.len() {
556:             let next = buffer.lines.remove(buffer.cursor_line + 1);
557:             if buffer.cursor_line + 1 < buffer.spans.len() {
558:                 buffer.spans.remove(buffer.cursor_line + 1);
559:             }
560:             buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].push_str(&next);
561:         } else {
562:             return;
563:         }
564:         buffer.dirty = true;
565:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
566:         self.version += 1;
567:         self.touch();
568:     }
569: 
570:     pub fn indent(&mut self) {
571:         if self.selection_range().is_some() {
572:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
573:                 buffer.remember(false);
574:             }
575:             self.indent_selection();
576:             return;
577:         }
578:         for _ in 0..4 {
579:             self.insert_char(' ');
580:         }
581:     }
582: 
583:     pub fn dedent(&mut self) {
584:         if self.selection_range().is_some() {
585:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
586:                 buffer.remember(false);
587:             }
588:             self.dedent_selection();
589:             return;
590:         }
591:         let remove = self
592:             .buffer()
593:             .map(|buffer| {
594:                 let line = &buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line];
595:                 if line.starts_with('\t') {
596:                     1
597:                 } else {
598:                     line.chars().take_while(|ch| *ch == ' ').count().min(4)
599:                 }
600:             })
601:             .unwrap_or(0);
602:         if remove == 0 {
603:             return;
604:         }
605:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
606:             buffer.remember(false);
607:         }
608:         self.delete_selection_internal();
609:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
610:             return;
611:         };
612:         let line = &mut buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line];
613:         let rest: String = line.chars().skip(remove).collect();
614:         *line = rest;
615:         buffer.cursor_col = buffer.cursor_col.saturating_sub(remove);
616:         buffer.dirty = true;
617:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
618:         self.version += 1;
619:         self.touch();
620:     }
621: 
622:     pub fn move_left(&mut self, extend: bool) {
623:         self.prepare_move(extend);
624:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
625:             if buffer.cursor_col > 0 {
626:                 buffer.cursor_col -= 1;
627:             } else if buffer.cursor_line > 0 {
628:                 buffer.cursor_line -= 1;
629:                 buffer.cursor_col = char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]);
630:             }
631:             self.touch();
632:         }
633:     }
634: 
635:     pub fn move_right(&mut self, extend: bool) {
636:         self.prepare_move(extend);
637:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
638:             let length = char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]);
639:             if buffer.cursor_col < length {
640:                 buffer.cursor_col += 1;
641:             } else if buffer.cursor_line + 1 < buffer.lines.len() {
642:                 buffer.cursor_line += 1;
643:                 buffer.cursor_col = 0;
644:             }
645:             self.touch();
646:         }
647:     }
648: 
649:     pub fn move_up(&mut self, extend: bool) {
650:         self.prepare_move(extend);
651:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
652:             if buffer.cursor_line > 0 {
653:                 buffer.cursor_line -= 1;
654:                 buffer.cursor_col = buffer
655:                     .cursor_col
656:                     .min(char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]));
657:             }
658:             self.touch();
659:         }
660:     }
661: 
662:     pub fn move_down(&mut self, extend: bool) {
663:         self.prepare_move(extend);
664:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
665:             if buffer.cursor_line + 1 < buffer.lines.len() {
666:                 buffer.cursor_line += 1;
667:                 buffer.cursor_col = buffer
668:                     .cursor_col
669:                     .min(char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]));
670:             }
671:             self.touch();
672:         }
673:     }
674: 
675:     pub fn move_home(&mut self, extend: bool) {
676:         self.prepare_move(extend);
677:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
678:             buffer.cursor_col = 0;
679:             self.touch();
680:         }
681:     }
682: 
683:     pub fn move_end(&mut self, extend: bool) {
684:         self.prepare_move(extend);
685:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
686:             buffer.cursor_col = char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]);
687:             self.touch();
688:         }
689:     }
690: 
691:     pub fn move_word_left(&mut self, extend: bool) {
692:         self.prepare_move(extend);
693:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
694:             let mut chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
695:             let mut col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
696:             while col > 0 && !is_word(chars[col - 1]) {
697:                 col -= 1;
698:             }
699:             while col > 0 && is_word(chars[col - 1]) {
700:                 col -= 1;
701:             }
702:             buffer.cursor_col = col;
703:             chars.clear();
704:             self.touch();
705:         }
706:     }
707: 
708:     pub fn move_word_right(&mut self, extend: bool) {
709:         self.prepare_move(extend);
710:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
711:             let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].chars().collect();
712:             let mut col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
713:             while col < chars.len() && is_word(chars[col]) {
714:                 col += 1;
715:             }
716:             while col < chars.len() && !is_word(chars[col]) {
717:                 col += 1;
718:             }
719:             buffer.cursor_col = col;
720:             self.touch();
721:         }
722:     }
723: 
724:     pub fn move_page(&mut self, lines: isize, extend: bool) {
725:         self.prepare_move(extend);
726:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
727:             let next = buffer.cursor_line as isize + lines;
728:             buffer.cursor_line =
729:                 next.clamp(0, buffer.lines.len().saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
730:             buffer.cursor_col = buffer
731:                 .cursor_col
732:                 .min(char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]));
733:             self.touch();
734:         }
735:     }
736: 
737:     pub fn doc_start(&mut self, extend: bool) {
738:         self.prepare_move(extend);
739:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
740:             buffer.cursor_line = 0;
741:             buffer.cursor_col = 0;
742:             self.touch();
743:         }
744:     }
745: 
746:     pub fn doc_end(&mut self, extend: bool) {
747:         self.prepare_move(extend);
748:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
749:             buffer.cursor_line = buffer.lines.len().saturating_sub(1);
750:             buffer.cursor_col = char_count(&buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line]);
751:             self.touch();
752:         }
753:     }
754: 
755:     pub fn switch_buffer(&mut self, delta: isize) {
756:         if self.buffers.len() < 2 {
757:             return;
758:         }
759:         let length = self.buffers.len() as isize;
760:         let next = ((self.active as isize + delta).rem_euclid(length)) as usize;
761:         self.select_buffer(next);
762:     }
763: 
764:     pub fn select_buffer(&mut self, index: usize) {
765:         if index >= self.buffers.len() {
766:             return;
767:         }
768:         self.active = index;
769:         self.buffers[self.active].undo_group = None;
770:         self.clear_prediction();
771:     }
772: 
773:     pub fn toggle_wrap(&mut self) -> bool {
774:         self.wrap = !self.wrap;
775:         self.wrap
776:     }
777: 
778:     pub fn visual_rows(&self, width: usize) -> Vec<VisualRow> {
779:         self.buffer()
780:             .map(|buffer| build_rows(&buffer.lines, self.wrap, width))
781:             .unwrap_or_default()
782:     }
783: 
784:     pub fn scroll_by(&mut self, delta: isize, width: usize) {
785:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffer() else {
786:             return;
787:         };
788:         let total = if self.wrap {
789:             build_rows(&buffer.lines, true, width).len()
790:         } else {
791:             buffer.lines.len()
792:         };
793:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
794:         let next = buffer.scroll as isize + delta;
795:         buffer.scroll = next.clamp(0, total.saturating_sub(1) as isize) as usize;
796:     }
797: 
798:     pub fn ensure_visible(&mut self, height: usize, width: usize) {
799:         if height == 0 || width == 0 {
800:             return;
801:         }
802:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffer() else {
803:             return;
804:         };
805: 
806:         if self.wrap {
807:             let rows = build_rows(&buffer.lines, true, width);
808:             let cursor_row = row_for_cursor(&rows, buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col);
809:             let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
810:             buffer.hscroll = 0;
811:             if cursor_row < buffer.scroll {
812:                 buffer.scroll = cursor_row;
813:             }
814:             if cursor_row >= buffer.scroll + height {
815:                 buffer.scroll = cursor_row + 1 - height;
816:             }
817:             buffer.scroll = buffer.scroll.min(rows.len().saturating_sub(1));
818:             return;
819:         }
820: 
821:         let cursor_line = buffer.cursor_line;
822:         let cursor_col = buffer.cursor_col;
823:         let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[cursor_line].chars().collect();
824:         let prefix: String = chars[..cursor_col.min(chars.len())].iter().collect();
825:         let column = display_width(&prefix);
826:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
827:         if cursor_line < buffer.scroll {
828:             buffer.scroll = cursor_line;
829:         }
830:         if cursor_line >= buffer.scroll + height {
831:             buffer.scroll = cursor_line + 1 - height;
832:         }
833:         if column < buffer.hscroll {
834:             buffer.hscroll = column;
835:         }
836:         if column >= buffer.hscroll + width {
837:             buffer.hscroll = column + 1 - width;
838:         }
839:     }
840: 
841:     pub fn cursor_visual(&self, width: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
842:         let buffer = self.buffer()?;
843:         let rows = build_rows(&buffer.lines, self.wrap, width);
844:         let row = row_for_cursor(&rows, buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col);
845:         let visual = rows.get(row)?;
846:         let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[visual.line].chars().collect();
847:         let start = visual.start.min(chars.len());
848:         let end = visual.end.min(chars.len()).max(start);
849:         let col = buffer.cursor_col.min(end).max(start);
850:         let prefix: String = chars[start..col].iter().collect();
851:         Some((row, display_width(&prefix)))
852:     }
853: 
854:     pub fn place_cursor_from_screen(&mut self, row: usize, cells: usize, width: usize) {
855:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffer() else {
856:             return;
857:         };
858:         let (line, start, end, target) = if self.wrap {
859:             let rows = build_rows(&buffer.lines, true, width);
860:             let visual = rows
861:                 .get(row)
862:                 .or_else(|| rows.last())
863:                 .copied()
864:                 .unwrap_or(VisualRow {
865:                     line: 0,
866:                     start: 0,
867:                     end: 0,
868:                 });
869:             (visual.line, visual.start, visual.end, cells)
870:         } else {
871:             let line = row.min(buffer.lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
872:             let end = char_count(&buffer.lines[line]);
873:             (line, 0, end, buffer.hscroll + cells)
874:         };
875:         let mut column = 0;
876:         let mut best = start;
877:         for (index, ch) in buffer.lines[line]
878:             .chars()
879:             .enumerate()
880:             .skip(start)
881:             .take(end.saturating_sub(start))
882:         {
883:             if column > target {
884:                 break;
885:             }
886:             best = index;
887:             if ch == '\t' {
888:                 column = (column / TAB + 1) * TAB;
889:             } else {
890:                 column += ch.width().unwrap_or(0);
891:             }
892:         }
893:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
894:         buffer.cursor_line = line;
895:         buffer.cursor_col = best;
896:         buffer.undo_group = None;
897:         self.touch();
898:     }
899: 
900:     pub fn tick(&mut self, now: Instant) {
901:         self.poll_highlight();
902:         let highlight_dirty = self
903:             .buffers
904:             .get(self.active)
905:             .map(|buffer| buffer.highlight_dirty)
906:             .unwrap_or(false);
907:         if highlight_dirty
908:             && self.highlight_inflight == 0
909:             && now.duration_since(self.last_edit) >= Duration::from_millis(250)
910:         {
911:             self.start_highlight();
912:         }
913: 
914:         if self.buffers.is_empty() {
915:             self.ghost = None;
916:             self.prediction_key = None;
917:             return;
918:         }
919: 
920:         if now.duration_since(self.last_edit) < Duration::from_millis(5) {
921:             return;
922:         }
923:         let buffer = &self.buffers[self.active];
924:         let key = (
925:             self.active,
926:             self.version,
927:             buffer.cursor_line,
928:             buffer.cursor_col,
929:         );
930:         if self.prediction_key == Some(key) {
931:             return;
932:         }
933:         self.prediction_key = Some(key);
934:         self.ghost = self.compute_ghost();
935:     }
936: 
937:     pub fn accept_prediction(&mut self) -> bool {
938:         if self.has_selection() {
939:             return false;
940:         }
941:         let Some(ghost) = self.ghost.take() else {
942:             return false;
943:         };
944:         if !ghost.contains('\n') {
945:             let learned = self.buffer().map(|buffer| {
946:                 let line = buffer.lines[buffer.cursor_line].clone();
947:                 let previous = buffer
948:                     .cursor_line
949:                     .checked_sub(1)
950:                     .and_then(|index| buffer.lines.get(index))
951:                     .cloned();
952:                 (previous, format!("{line}{ghost}"))
953:             });
954:             if let Some((previous, full)) = learned {
955:                 self.predictor.learn(&full);
956:                 if let Some(previous) = previous {
957:                     self.predictor.learn_transition(&previous, &full);
958:                 }
959:             }
960:         }
961:         if !ghost.contains('\n') {
962:             for ch in ghost.chars() {
963:                 self.insert_char(ch);
964:             }
965:             return true;
966:         }
967:         self.insert_raw(&ghost);
968:         true
969:     }
970: 
971:     fn insert_raw(&mut self, text: &str) {
972:         if self.buffers.get(self.active).is_none() {
973:             return;
974:         }
975:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
976:             buffer.remember(false);
977:         }
978:         self.delete_selection_internal();
979:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) else {
980:             return;
981:         };
982:         let line = buffer.cursor_line;
983:         let chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[line].chars().collect();
984:         let col = buffer.cursor_col.min(chars.len());
985:         let before: String = chars[..col].iter().collect();
986:         let after: String = chars[col..].iter().collect();
987:         let parts: Vec<&str> = text.split('\n').collect();
988:         let last = parts.len().saturating_sub(1);
989:         buffer.lines[line] = format!("{before}{}", parts[0]);
990:         if last == 0 {
991:             buffer.lines[line].push_str(&after);
992:             buffer.cursor_col = col + parts[0].chars().count();
993:         } else {
994:             let mut inserted: Vec<String> = parts[1..last]
995:                 .iter()
996:                 .map(|part| (*part).to_string())
997:                 .collect();
998:             inserted.push(format!("{}{}", parts[last], after));
999:             let added = inserted.len();
1000:             buffer.lines.splice(line + 1..line + 1, inserted);
1001:             if line < buffer.spans.len() {
1002:                 buffer.spans.splice(
1003:                     line + 1..line + 1,
1004:                     std::iter::repeat(Vec::new()).take(added),
1005:                 );
1006:             }
1007:             buffer.cursor_line = line + added;
1008:             buffer.cursor_col = parts[last].chars().count();
1009:         }
1010:         buffer.dirty = true;
1011:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
1012:         self.version += 1;
1013:         self.touch();
1014:     }
1015: 
1016:     fn compute_ghost(&self) -> Option<String> {
1017:         let buffer = self.buffers.get(self.active)?;
1018:         if buffer.selection.is_some() {
1019:             return None;
1020:         }
1021:         let line = buffer.lines.get(buffer.cursor_line)?;
1022:         if buffer.cursor_col != char_count(line) {
1023:             return None;
1024:         }
1025:         if buffer.lines.len() > 60_000 {
1026:             return None;
1027:         }
1028: 
1029:         let indent: String = line
1030:             .chars()
1031:             .take_while(|ch| *ch == ' ' || *ch == '\t')
1032:             .collect();
1033:         let previous = buffer
1034:             .cursor_line
1035:             .checked_sub(1)
1036:             .and_then(|index| buffer.lines.get(index))
1037:             .map(String::as_str);
1038: 
1039:         let mut corpus: Vec<&str> = buffer.lines.iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
1040:         for (index, other) in self.buffers.iter().enumerate() {
1041:             if index == self.active {
1042:                 continue;
1043:             }
1044:             for candidate in &other.lines {
1045:                 corpus.push(candidate);
1046:                 if corpus.len() >= 40_000 {
1047:                     break;
1048:                 }
1049:             }
1050:             if corpus.len() >= 40_000 {
1051:                 break;
1052:             }
1053:         }
1054: 
1055:         self.predictor
1056:             .suggest(&corpus, line, buffer.cursor_col, previous, &indent)
1057:     }
1058: 
1059:     fn start_highlight(&mut self) {
1060:         let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get(self.active) else {
1061:             return;
1062:         };
1063:         let index = self.active;
1064:         let version = self.version;
1065:         let path = buffer.path.clone();
1066:         let lines = buffer.lines.clone();
1067:         let highlighter = Arc::clone(&self.highlighter);
1068:         let tx = self.highlight_tx.clone();
1069:         self.highlight_inflight += 1;
1070:         thread::spawn(move || {
1071:             let spans = highlighter.highlight(&path, &lines);
1072:             let _ = tx.send(HighlightResult {
1073:                 index,
1074:                 version,
1075:                 spans,
1076:             });
1077:         });
1078:     }
1079: 
1080:     fn poll_highlight(&mut self) {
1081:         while let Ok(result) = self.highlight_rx.try_recv() {
1082:             self.highlight_inflight = self.highlight_inflight.saturating_sub(1);
1083:             if result.version != self.version {
1084:                 continue;
1085:             }
1086:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(result.index) {
1087:                 buffer.spans = result.spans;
1088:                 buffer.highlight_dirty = false;
1089:             }
1090:         }
1091:     }
1092: 
1093:     fn prepare_move(&mut self, extend: bool) {
1094:         if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
1095:             buffer.undo_group = None;
1096:             if extend {
1097:                 if buffer.selection.is_none() {
1098:                     buffer.selection = Some((buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col));
1099:                 }
1100:             } else {
1101:                 buffer.selection = None;
1102:             }
1103:         }
1104:     }
1105: 
1106:     fn selection_range(&self) -> Option<((usize, usize), (usize, usize))> {
1107:         let buffer = self.buffer()?;
1108:         let anchor = buffer.selection?;
1109:         let line_count = buffer.lines.len();
1110:         if line_count == 0 {
1111:             return None;
1112:         }
1113:         let anchor_line = anchor.0.min(line_count - 1);
1114:         let anchor_col = anchor.1.min(char_count(&buffer.lines[anchor_line]));
1115:         let start = (anchor_line, anchor_col);
1116:         let end = (buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col);
1117:         if start == end {
1118:             None
1119:         } else if start < end {
1120:             Some((start, end))
1121:         } else {
1122:             Some((end, start))
1123:         }
1124:     }
1125: 
1126:     fn delete_selection_internal(&mut self) -> bool {
1127:         let Some((start, end)) = self.selection_range() else {
1128:             if let Some(buffer) = self.buffers.get_mut(self.active) {
1129:                 buffer.selection = None;
1130:             }
1131:             return false;
1132:         };
1133: 
1134:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
1135:         let start_chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[start.0].chars().collect();
1136:         let end_chars: Vec<char> = buffer.lines[end.0].chars().collect();
1137:         let start_col = start.1.min(start_chars.len());
1138:         let end_col = end.1.min(end_chars.len());
1139:         let prefix: String = start_chars[..start_col].iter().collect();
1140:         let suffix: String = end_chars[end_col..].iter().collect();
1141:         let merged = format!("{prefix}{suffix}");
1142:         buffer
1143:             .lines
1144:             .splice(start.0..=end.0, std::iter::once(merged));
1145:         if start.0 < buffer.spans.len() {
1146:             let remove_end = end.0.min(buffer.spans.len() - 1);
1147:             buffer
1148:                 .spans
1149:                 .splice(start.0..=remove_end, std::iter::once(Vec::new()));
1150:         }
1151:         buffer.cursor_line = start.0;
1152:         buffer.cursor_col = prefix.chars().count();
1153:         buffer.selection = None;
1154:         true
1155:     }
1156: 
1157:     fn indent_selection(&mut self) {
1158:         let Some((start, end)) = self.selection_range() else {
1159:             return;
1160:         };
1161:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
1162:         for index in start.0..=end.0 {
1163:             buffer.lines[index].insert_str(0, "    ");
1164:         }
1165:         buffer.cursor_col += 4;
1166:         if let Some(anchor) = buffer.selection.as_mut() {
1167:             if anchor.0 >= start.0 && anchor.0 <= end.0 {
1168:                 anchor.1 += 4;
1169:             }
1170:         }
1171:         buffer.dirty = true;
1172:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
1173:         self.version += 1;
1174:         self.touch();
1175:     }
1176: 
1177:     fn dedent_selection(&mut self) {
1178:         let Some((start, end)) = self.selection_range() else {
1179:             return;
1180:         };
1181:         let buffer = &mut self.buffers[self.active];
1182:         let cursor_line = buffer.cursor_line;
1183:         let mut cursor_removed = 0;
1184:         for index in start.0..=end.0 {
1185:             let remove = {
1186:                 let line = &buffer.lines[index];
1187:                 if line.starts_with('\t') {
1188:                     1
1189:                 } else {
1190:                     line.chars().take_while(|ch| *ch == ' ').count().min(4)
1191:                 }
1192:             };
1193:             if remove > 0 {
1194:                 buffer.lines[index] = buffer.lines[index].chars().skip(remove).collect();
1195:             }
1196:             if index == cursor_line {
1197:                 cursor_removed = remove;
1198:             }
1199:             if let Some(anchor) = buffer.selection.as_mut() {
1200:                 if anchor.0 == index {
1201:                     anchor.1 = anchor.1.saturating_sub(remove);
1202:                 }
1203:             }
1204:         }
1205:         buffer.cursor_col = buffer.cursor_col.saturating_sub(cursor_removed);
1206:         buffer.dirty = true;
1207:         buffer.highlight_dirty = true;
1208:         self.version += 1;
1209:         self.touch();
1210:     }
1211: 
1212:     fn clear_prediction(&mut self) {
1213:         self.ghost = None;
1214:         self.prediction_key = None;
1215:         self.last_edit = Instant::now();
1216:     }
1217: 
1218:     fn touch(&mut self) {
1219:         self.ghost = None;
1220:         self.prediction_key = None;
1221:         self.last_edit = Instant::now();
1222:     }
1223: }
1224: 
1225: pub fn display_width(text: &str) -> usize {
1226:     let mut column = 0;
1227:     for ch in text.chars() {
1228:         if ch == '\t' {
1229:             column = (column / TAB + 1) * TAB;
1230:         } else {
1231:             column += ch.width().unwrap_or(0);
1232:         }
1233:     }
1234:     column
1235: }
1236: 
1237: pub fn relative_label(root: &Path, path: &Path) -> String {
1238:     let relative = path
1239:         .strip_prefix(root)
1240:         .map(|value| value.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1241:         .unwrap_or_else(|_| path.display().to_string());
1242:     if relative.is_empty() {
1243:         path.file_name()
1244:             .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1245:             .unwrap_or(relative)
1246:     } else {
1247:         relative
1248:     }
1249: }
1250: 
1251: fn char_count(text: &str) -> usize {
1252:     text.chars().count()
1253: }
1254: 
1255: fn row_for_cursor(rows: &[VisualRow], line: usize, col: usize) -> usize {
1256:     let mut last = 0;
1257:     for (index, row) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
1258:         if row.line == line {
1259:             last = index;
1260:             if col >= row.start && col < row.end {
1261:                 return index;
1262:             }
1263:         }
1264:     }
1265:     last
1266: }
1267: 
1268: fn build_rows(lines: &[String], wrap: bool, width: usize) -> Vec<VisualRow> {
1269:     let mut rows = Vec::new();
1270:     for (line_index, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
1271:         let chars: Vec<char> = line.chars().collect();
1272:         if !wrap || width == 0 || chars.is_empty() {
1273:             rows.push(VisualRow {
1274:                 line: line_index,
1275:                 start: 0,
1276:                 end: chars.len(),
1277:             });
1278:             continue;
1279:         }
1280: 
1281:         let mut start = 0;
1282:         while start < chars.len() {
1283:             let mut used = 0;
1284:             let mut last_break = None;
1285:             let mut index = start;
1286:             while index < chars.len() {
1287:                 let char_width = if chars[index] == '\t' {
1288:                     TAB - used % TAB
1289:                 } else {
1290:                     chars[index].width().unwrap_or(0)
1291:                 };
1292:                 if used + char_width > width && index > start {
1293:                     break;
1294:                 }
1295:                 used += char_width;
1296:                 index += 1;
1297:                 if chars[index - 1].is_whitespace() {
1298:                     last_break = Some(index);
1299:                 }
1300:             }
1301: 
1302:             let end = if index < chars.len() {
1303:                 last_break.filter(|end| *end > start).unwrap_or(index)
1304:             } else {
1305:                 index
1306:             };
1307:             rows.push(VisualRow {
1308:                 line: line_index,
1309:                 start,
1310:                 end,
1311:             });
1312:             start = end;
1313:         }
1314:     }
1315:     rows
1316: }
1317: 
1318: fn is_word(ch: char) -> bool {
1319:     ch.is_alphanumeric() || ch == '_'
1320: }
1321: 
1322: fn spans_match_line(line: &str, spans: &[(Style, String)]) -> bool {
1323:     let mut chars = line.chars();
1324:     for (_, text) in spans {
1325:         for ch in text.chars() {
1326:             if chars.next() != Some(ch) {
1327:                 return false;
1328:             }
1329:         }
1330:     }
1331:     chars.next().is_none()
1332: }

1490: fn read_text(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
1491:     let metadata = fs::metadata(path).map_err(|error| format!("{}: {error}", path.display()))?;
1492:     if metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_BYTES {
1493:         return Err("file is too large to open".into());
1494:     }
1495:     let bytes = fs::read(path).map_err(|error| format!("{}: {error}", path.display()))?;
1496:     if bytes.contains(&0) {
1497:         return Err("binary file".into());
1498:     }
1499:     let text = String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| "file is not utf-8".to_string())?;
1500:     Ok(text
1501:         .split('\n')
1502:         .map(|line| line.strip_suffix('\r').unwrap_or(line).to_string())
1503:         .collect())
1504: }

    fn refresh_menu_page(&mut self) {
        let Some(page) = self.menu.as_ref().and_then(|menu| menu.pages.last()) else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(kind) = page.kind else {
            return;
        };
        let selected = page.selected;
        let mut refreshed = self.menu_page(kind);
        refreshed.selected = selected.min(refreshed.items.len().saturating_sub(1));
        if let Some(page) = self.menu.as_mut().and_then(|menu| menu.pages.last_mut()) {
            *page = refreshed;
        }
    }

    fn context_items(is_dir: bool, expanded: bool) -> Vec<ContextMenuItem> {
        let first = if is_dir {
            if expanded {
                "Collapse folder"
            } else {
                "Expand folder"
            }
        } else {
            "Open"
        };
        vec![
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: first,
                hint: "enter",
                action: ContextAction::Open,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "Open in default app",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::OpenDefault,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "New file",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::NewFile,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "New folder",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::NewFolder,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "Rename",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::Rename,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "Delete",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::Delete,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "Copy path",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::CopyPath,
            },
            ContextMenuItem {
                label: "Copy relative path",
                hint: "",
                action: ContextAction::CopyRelativePath,
            },
        ]
    }

    fn open_context_menu(&mut self, x: u16, y: u16) {
        let Some(node) = self.tree.selected_node() else {
            return;
        };
        let target = node.path.clone();
        let is_dir = node.is_dir;
        let expanded = node.expanded;
        self.context_menu = Some(ContextMenu {
            target,
            is_dir,
            selected: 0,
            anchor: (x, y),
            items: Self::context_items(is_dir, expanded),
        });
    }

    fn handle_context_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent) {
        let Some(menu) = self.context_menu.as_mut() else {
            return;
        };
        match key.code {
            KeyCode::Esc => self.context_menu = None,
            KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => menu.selected = menu.selected.saturating_sub(1),
            KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => {
                menu.selected = (menu.selected + 1).min(menu.items.len().saturating_sub(1));
            }
            KeyCode::Home => menu.selected = 0,
            KeyCode::End => menu.selected = menu.items.len().saturating_sub(1),
            KeyCode::Enter => {
                if let Some(action) = menu.items.get(menu.selected).map(|item| item.action) {
                    self.run_context_action(action);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn open_create_dialog(&mut self, parent: PathBuf, is_dir: bool) {
        let kind = if is_dir { "folder" } else { "file" };
        let location = self.editor.rel_of(&parent);
        self.dialog = Some(FileDialog::Create {
            parent,
            is_dir,
            prompt: TextPrompt::new(format!("new {kind} in {location}"), ""),
        });
    }

    fn run_context_action(&mut self, action: ContextAction) {
        let Some(menu) = self.context_menu.take() else {
            return;
        };
        let target = menu.target;
        let is_dir = menu.is_dir;
        match action {
            ContextAction::Open => {
                if is_dir {
                    self.tree.toggle_selected();
                } else {
                    self.open_file(&target);
                }
            }
            ContextAction::OpenDefault => match open_in_default_app(&target) {

    fn handle_agent_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent, ctrl: bool, alt: bool) {
        let running = matches!(self.pi, PiState::Running(_));
        let suggestions = !self.agent.suggestions.is_empty();
        match key.code {
            KeyCode::PageUp => self.agent.scroll_by(5),
            KeyCode::PageDown => self.agent.scroll_by(-5),
            KeyCode::Esc => {
                if suggestions {
                    self.agent.clear_suggestions();
                } else if self.agent.busy {
                    self.abort_agent();
                }
            }
            KeyCode::Up if suggestions => self.agent.move_suggestion(-1),
            KeyCode::Down if suggestions => self.agent.move_suggestion(1),
            KeyCode::Tab if suggestions => {
                self.agent.accept_suggestion();
            }
            KeyCode::Enter if suggestions => {
                self.agent.accept_suggestion();
            }
            KeyCode::Enter => self.send_prompt(),
            _ if !running => {}
            KeyCode::Backspace => {
                self.agent.backspace_input();
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::Delete => {
                self.agent.delete_input();
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::Left => {
                self.agent.move_input(-1);
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::Right => {
                self.agent.move_input(1);
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::Home => {
                self.agent.input_home();
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::End => {
                self.agent.input_end();
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => self.agent.clear_input(),
            KeyCode::Char(ch) if !ctrl && !alt => {
                self.agent.insert_input(ch);
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn save(&mut self) {
        if self.unsupported.is_some() {
            self.set_status("this file type is not supported");
            return;
        }
        match self.editor.save_active() {
            Ok(message) => self.set_status(message),
            Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
        }
    }

    fn copy_text(&mut self, text: &str, action: &str) {
        self.editor.clipboard = text.to_string();
        match copy_to_clipboard(text) {
            Ok(()) => self.set_status(action),
            Err(_) => self.set_status(format!("{action} to app clipboard")),
        }
    }

    fn clipboard_text(&self) -> Option<String> {
        clipboard_from_system()
            .filter(|text| !text.is_empty())
            .or_else(|| (!self.editor.clipboard.is_empty()).then(|| self.editor.clipboard.clone()))
    }

    fn paste_editor(&mut self) {
        match self.clipboard_text() {
            Some(text) => {
                self.editor.insert_text(&text);
                self.set_status("pasted");
            }
            None => self.set_status("clipboard is empty"),
        }
    }

    fn paste_agent(&mut self) {
        match self.clipboard_text() {
            Some(text) => {
                for ch in text.replace(['\r', '\n'], " ").chars() {
                    self.agent.insert_input(ch);
                }
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
                self.set_status("pasted");
            }
            None => self.set_status("clipboard is empty"),
        }
    }

    pub fn open_path(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        self.open_file(path);
    }

    pub fn status_hint(&self, width: u16) -> String {
        let full = width >= 110;
        if let Some(dialog) = &self.dialog {
            return match dialog {
                FileDialog::Delete { .. } => "enter delete  esc cancel",
                FileDialog::Rename { .. } | FileDialog::Create { .. } => {
                    "enter confirm  esc cancel"
                }
            }
            .to_string();
        }
        if self.context_menu.is_some() {
            return "up/down select  enter run  esc close".to_string();
        }
        if let Some(menu) = &self.menu {
            return if menu.pages.len() > 1 {
                "enter run  esc back"
            } else {
                "enter open  esc close"
            }
            .to_string();
        }
        if self.diff.is_some() {
            return "j/k scroll  esc close".to_string();
        }
        if self.unsupported.is_some() {
            return if full {
                "enter open in default app  esc close"
            } else {
                "enter open  esc close"
            }
            .to_string();
        }

        let sidebar_toggle = if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Git {
            "ctrl+g files"
        } else {
            "ctrl+g git"
        };
        let agent_toggle = if self.agent_hidden {
            "ctrl+b show"
        } else {
            "ctrl+b agent"
        };

        if self.focus == Focus::Tree {
            return match self.sidebar {
                Sidebar::Git if self.git.input_focus => {
                    if full {
                        "enter commit  esc cancel"
                    } else {
                        "enter commit"
                    }
                }
                Sidebar::Git => {
                    if full {
                        "enter diff  o open  c commit  p push  d discard  r refresh  ctrl+g files"
                    } else {
                        "enter diff  c commit  p push  d discard  ctrl+g files"
                    }
                }
                Sidebar::Files => {
                    if full {
                        return format!(
                            "enter open  right-click menu  h/l fold  {sidebar_toggle}  {agent_toggle}  ctrl+q quit"
                        );
                    }
                    return format!("enter open  right-click menu  {sidebar_toggle}  ctrl+q quit");
                }
            }
            .to_string();
        }

        if self.focus == Focus::Agent {
            if !self.agent.suggestions.is_empty() {
                return if full {
                    "up/down select  tab/enter complete  esc close"
                } else {
                    "tab complete  esc close"
                }
                .to_string();
            }
            return match &self.pi {
                PiState::Running(_) if self.agent.busy => {
                    if full {
                        "enter queue  esc stop  ctrl+b hide"
                    } else {
                        "esc stop  ctrl+b hide"
                    }
                }
                PiState::Running(_) => {
                    if full {
                        "enter send  /help commands  ctrl+b hide"
                    } else {
                        "enter send  ctrl+b hide"
                    }
                }
                PiState::Missing | PiState::Failed(_) => {
                    if full {
                        "pi unavailable  ctrl+b hide  ctrl+g files"
                    } else {
                        "ctrl+b hide"
                    }
                }
            }
            .to_string();
        }

        match self.editor.buffer() {
            Some(buffer) if buffer.dirty => {
                if full {
                    format!(
                        "ctrl+s save  ctrl+z undo  ctrl+n tab  ctrl+w close  ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}"
                    )
                } else {
                    format!("ctrl+s save  ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}")
                }
            }
            Some(_) => {
                if full {
                    format!(
                        "ctrl+z undo  ctrl+n tab  ctrl+w close  ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}  {agent_toggle}"
                    )
                } else {
                    format!("ctrl+z undo  ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}  ctrl+q quit")
                }
            }
            None => {
                if full {
                    format!(
                        "select a file  ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}  {agent_toggle}  ctrl+q quit"
                    )
                } else {
                    format!("ctrl+p menu  {sidebar_toggle}  ctrl+q quit")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn toggle_agent(&mut self) {
        self.set_agent_hidden(!self.agent_hidden);
    }

    pub fn set_agent_hidden(&mut self, hidden: bool) {
        self.agent_hidden = hidden;
        if hidden && self.focus == Focus::Agent {
            self.focus = Focus::Editor;
        }
    }

    fn open_file(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        self.diff = None;
        if is_media_file(path) {
            self.show_unsupported(path);
            return;
        }
        match self.editor.open(path) {
            Ok(()) => {
                self.unsupported = None;
                self.focus = Focus::Editor;
                self.follow_editor_cursor = true;
                self.tree.reveal(path);
            }
            Err(error) if is_unsupported_error(&error) => self.show_unsupported(path),
            Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
        }
    }

    fn show_unsupported(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        self.unsupported = Some(path.to_path_buf());
        self.focus = Focus::Editor;
        self.tree.reveal(path);
    }

    fn open_unsupported_in_default(&mut self) {
        let Some(path) = self.unsupported.clone() else {
            return;
        };
        match open_in_default_app(&path) {
            Ok(()) => self.set_status("opened in default app"),
            Err(error) => self.set_status(error),
        }
    }

    fn cycle_focus(&mut self) {
        self.focus = match self.focus {
            Focus::Tree => Focus::Editor,
            Focus::Editor if self.agent_hidden => Focus::Tree,
            Focus::Editor => Focus::Agent,
            Focus::Agent => Focus::Tree,
        };
    }

    pub fn handle_paste(&mut self, text: &str) {
        match self.focus {
            Focus::Editor => self.editor.insert_text(text),
            Focus::Agent => {
                let text = text.replace(['\r', '\n'], " ");
                for ch in text.chars() {
                    self.agent.insert_input(ch);
                }
                self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
            }
            Focus::Tree => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn handle_mouse(&mut self, mouse: MouseEvent) {
        let x = mouse.column;
        let y = mouse.row;
        self.hover = Some((x, y));
        if self.dialog.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        if self.context_menu.is_some() {
            self.refresh_hover();
            match mouse.kind {
                MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left) => {
                    let rect = self.ui.context_menu;
                    if inside(rect, x, y)
                        && y > rect.y
                        && y + 1 < rect.y.saturating_add(rect.height)
                    {
                        let row = y.saturating_sub(rect.y + 1) as usize;
                        if let Some(menu) = &mut self.context_menu {
                            if row < menu.items.len() {
                                menu.selected = row;
                            }
                        }
                        let action = self
                            .context_menu
                            .as_ref()
                            .and_then(|menu| menu.items.get(row))
                            .map(|item| item.action);
                        if let Some(action) = action {
                            self.run_context_action(action);
                        }
                    } else {
                        self.context_menu = None;
                    }
                }
                MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Right) => {
                    if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Files && self.tree_hover_row(x, y).is_some() {
                        let row = y.saturating_sub(self.ui.tree_body.y) as usize;
                        self.tree.select_row(row);
                        self.focus = Focus::Tree;
                        self.open_context_menu(x, y);
                    } else {
                        self.context_menu = None;
                    }
                }
                MouseEventKind::Moved => {
                    let rect = self.ui.context_menu;
                    if inside(rect, x, y)
                        && y > rect.y
                        && y + 1 < rect.y.saturating_add(rect.height)
                    {
                        let row = y.saturating_sub(rect.y + 1) as usize;
                        if let Some(menu) = &mut self.context_menu {
                            if row < menu.items.len() {
                                menu.selected = row;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                MouseEventKind::ScrollUp => {
                    if let Some(menu) = &mut self.context_menu {
                        menu.selected = menu.selected.saturating_sub(1);
                    }
                }
                MouseEventKind::ScrollDown => {
                    if let Some(menu) = &mut self.context_menu {
                        menu.selected = (menu.selected + 1).min(menu.items.len().saturating_sub(1));
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
            return;
        }
        if self.menu.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        self.refresh_hover();

        if self.diff.is_some() {
            let in_editor = inside(self.ui.editor_text, x, y);
            match mouse.kind {
                MouseEventKind::ScrollUp if in_editor => {
                    if let Some(diff) = &mut self.diff {
                        diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_sub(3);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                MouseEventKind::ScrollDown if in_editor => {
                    if let Some(diff) = &mut self.diff {
                        diff.scroll = diff.scroll.saturating_add(3);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left) if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) => {}
                MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Right) if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) => {}
                _ => return,
            }
        }

        match mouse.kind {
            MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Right) => {
                if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Files && self.tree_hover_row(x, y).is_some() {
                    let row = y.saturating_sub(self.ui.tree_body.y) as usize;
                    self.tree.select_row(row);
                    self.focus = Focus::Tree;
                    self.open_context_menu(x, y);
                }
            }
            MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left) => {
                self.git.confirm_discard = false;
                self.dragging = false;
                if let Some(edge) = self.resize_edge_at(x, y) {
                    self.resizing = Some(edge);
                    self.hover_edge = Some(edge);
                    self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Resize);
                    return;
                }
                if !inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) {
                    self.last_tree_click = None;
                }
                if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) {
                    self.focus = Focus::Tree;
                    if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Git {
                        if inside(self.ui.git_input, x, y) {
                            self.git.input_focus = true;
                        } else if y >= self.ui.tree_body.y {
                            if self.git_hover_row(x, y).is_some() {
                                self.git
                                    .select_row(y.saturating_sub(self.ui.tree_body.y) as usize);
                                self.open_diff();
                            }
                        }
                    } else if y >= self.ui.tree_body.y {
                        let hover = self.tree_hover_row(x, y);
                        let double = self.is_double_tree_click(hover);
                        if double && hover.is_none() {
                            self.open_create_dialog(self.root.clone(), false);
                            return;
                        }
                        let row = y.saturating_sub(self.ui.tree_body.y) as usize;
                        self.tree.select_row(row);
                        if let Some(path) = self.tree.activate() {
                            self.open_file(&path);
                        }
                    }
                } else if inside(self.ui.editor, x, y) {
                    self.focus = Focus::Editor;
                    self.follow_editor_cursor = true;
                    if inside(self.ui.unsupported_button, x, y) {
                        self.open_unsupported_in_default();
                        return;
                    }
                    let tab = self
                        .ui
                        .tabs
                        .iter()
                        .find(|(rect, _)| inside(*rect, x, y))
                        .map(|(_, index)| *index);
                    if let Some(index) = tab {
                        self.editor.select_buffer(index);
                    } else if inside(self.ui.editor_text, x, y) {
                        let (row, cells, width) = self.editor_position(x, y);
                        self.editor.begin_mouse_selection(row, cells, width);
                        self.dragging = true;
                    }
                } else if inside(self.ui.agent, x, y) {
                    self.focus = Focus::Agent;
                    if let Some(index) = self.thinking_hover_entry(x, y) {
                        if self.agent.toggle_thinking(index) {
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    if inside(self.ui.agent_input, x, y) {
                        self.agent.input_end();
                        self.refresh_agent_suggestions();
                    }
                }
            }
            MouseEventKind::Drag(MouseButton::Left) => {
                if let Some(edge) = self.resizing {
                    self.resize_drag(edge, x);
                    return;
                }
                if self.dragging && inside(self.ui.editor_text, x, y) {
                    let (row, cells, width) = self.editor_position(x, y);
                    self.editor.extend_mouse_selection(row, cells, width);
                }
            }
            MouseEventKind::Up(MouseButton::Left) => {
                self.dragging = false;
                self.resizing = None;
                self.refresh_hover();
            }
            MouseEventKind::ScrollUp => {
                if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) {
                    match self.sidebar {
                        Sidebar::Files => self.tree.move_selection(-1),
                        Sidebar::Git => self.git.move_selection(-1),
                    }
                } else if inside(self.ui.editor, x, y) {
                    let width = self.ui.editor_text.width.saturating_sub(self.ui.gutter) as usize;
                    self.follow_editor_cursor = false;
                    self.editor.scroll_by(-3, width);
                } else if inside(self.ui.agent, x, y) {
                    self.agent.scroll_by(3);
                }
            }
            MouseEventKind::ScrollDown => {
                if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) {
                    match self.sidebar {
                        Sidebar::Files => self.tree.move_selection(1),
                        Sidebar::Git => self.git.move_selection(1),
                    }
                } else if inside(self.ui.editor, x, y) {
                    let width = self.ui.editor_text.width.saturating_sub(self.ui.gutter) as usize;
                    self.follow_editor_cursor = false;
                    self.editor.scroll_by(3, width);
                } else if inside(self.ui.agent, x, y) {
                    self.agent.scroll_by(-3);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

impl App {
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub fn pointer(&self) -> PointerShape {
        self.pointer
    }

    pub fn take_pointer_update(&mut self) -> Option<PointerShape> {
        if self.pointer_dirty {
            self.pointer_dirty = false;
            Some(self.pointer)
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    pub fn refresh_hover(&mut self) {
        let Some((x, y)) = self.hover else {
            self.hover_edge = None;
            self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Default);
            return;
        };

        if self.dialog.is_some() {
            self.hover_edge = None;
            self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Default);
            return;
        }

        if self.context_menu.is_some() {
            self.hover_edge = None;
            let rect = self.ui.context_menu;
            let row = y.saturating_sub(rect.y + 1) as usize;
            let over_item = inside(rect, x, y)
                && y > rect.y
                && y + 1 < rect.y.saturating_add(rect.height)
                && self
                    .context_menu
                    .as_ref()
                    .is_some_and(|menu| row < menu.items.len());
            self.set_pointer(if over_item {
                PointerShape::Pointer
            } else {
                PointerShape::Default
            });
            return;
        }

        if self.diff.is_some() {
            self.hover_edge = None;
            let over_row = match self.sidebar {
                Sidebar::Files => self.tree_hover_row(x, y).is_some(),
                Sidebar::Git => self.git_hover_row(x, y).is_some(),
            };
            if over_row {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Pointer);
            } else {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Default);
            }
            return;
        }

        let edge = self.resize_edge_at(x, y);
        self.hover_edge = edge;
        if edge.is_some() || self.resizing.is_some() {
            self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Resize);
            return;
        }

        if inside(self.ui.tree, x, y) {
            if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Git && inside(self.ui.git_input, x, y) {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Text);
            } else if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Files && self.tree_hover_row(x, y).is_some() {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Pointer);
            } else if self.sidebar == Sidebar::Git && self.git_hover_row(x, y).is_some() {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Pointer);
            } else {
                self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Default);
            }
            return;
        }

        if inside(self.ui.unsupported_button, x, y)
            || self.ui.tabs.iter().any(|(rect, _)| inside(*rect, x, y))
            || self.thinking_hover_entry(x, y).is_some()
        {
            self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Pointer);
        } else if inside(self.ui.editor_text, x, y) || inside(self.ui.agent_input, x, y) {
            self.set_pointer(PointerShape::Text);

91:     let border = if matches!(app.resizing, Some(ResizeEdge::Sidebar)) {
92:         theme::ACCENT
93:     } else if app.hover_edge == Some(ResizeEdge::Sidebar) {
94:         theme::DIM
95:     } else {

209: fn draw_tree_rows(frame: &mut Frame, body: Rect, app: &mut App) {
210:     let height = body.height as usize;
211:     if height == 0 {
212:         return;
213:     }
214: 
215:     if app.tree.visible.is_empty() {
216:         frame.render_widget(
217:             Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
218:                 "empty project",
219:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
220:             )))
221:             .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
222:             body,
223:         );
224:         return;
225:     }
226: 
227:     if app.tree.selected < app.tree.scroll {
228:         app.tree.scroll = app.tree.selected;
229:     }
230:     if app.tree.selected >= app.tree.scroll + height {
231:         app.tree.scroll = app.tree.selected + 1 - height;
232:     }
233: 
234:     let focused = app.focus == Focus::Tree;
235:     let width = body.width as usize;
236:     let hover_row = app.hover.and_then(|(x, y)| {
237:         (x >= body.x && x < body.x + body.width && y >= body.y && y < body.y + body.height)
238:             .then_some((y - body.y) as usize + app.tree.scroll)
239:     });
240:     let mut lines = Vec::with_capacity(height);
241: 
242:     for position in app.tree.scroll..app.tree.visible.len().min(app.tree.scroll + height) {
243:         let node = &app.tree.nodes[app.tree.visible[position]];
244:         let selected = position == app.tree.selected;
245:         let hovered = hover_row == Some(position);
246:         let bg = if selected {
247:             if focused {
248:                 theme::SELECT
249:             } else {
250:                 theme::PANEL_ALT
251:             }
252:         } else if hovered {
253:             theme::HOVER
254:         } else {
255:             theme::PANEL
256:         };
257: 
258:         let indent = "  ".repeat(node.depth);
259:         let marker = if node.is_dir {
260:             if node.expanded {
261:                 "▾ "
262:             } else {
263:                 "▸ "
264:             }
265:         } else {
266:             ""
267:         };
268:         let prefix = format!("{indent}{marker}");
269:         let prefix_width = display_width(&prefix);
270:         let name_width = width.saturating_sub(prefix_width);
271:         let name = clip_display(&node.name, name_width);
272:         let name_width = display_width(&name);
273: 
274:         let mut spans = vec![
275:             Span::styled(prefix.clone(), Style::default().fg(theme::DIM).bg(bg)),
276:             Span::styled(name, Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(bg)),
277:         ];
278:         let used = prefix_width + name_width;
279:         if used < width {
280:             spans.push(Span::styled(
281:                 " ".repeat(width - used),
282:                 Style::default().bg(bg),
283:             ));
284:         }
285:         lines.push(Line::from(spans));
286:     }
287: 
288:     frame.render_widget(
289:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
290:         body,
291:     );
292: }

294: fn draw_git_rows(frame: &mut Frame, body: Rect, app: &mut App) {
295:     let height = body.height as usize;
296:     if height == 0 {
297:         return;
298:     }
299:
