329:             body,
330:         );
331:         return;
332:     }
333: 
334:     if app.git.selected < app.git.scroll {
335:         app.git.scroll = app.git.selected;
336:     }
337:     if app.git.selected >= app.git.scroll + height {
338:         app.git.scroll = app.git.selected + 1 - height;
339:     }
340: 
341:     let focused = app.focus == Focus::Tree;
342:     let width = body.width as usize;
343:     let mut lines = Vec::with_capacity(height);
344: 
345:     for position in app.git.scroll..app.git.changes.len().min(app.git.scroll + height) {
346:         let change = &app.git.changes[position];
347:         let selected = position == app.git.selected;
348:         let bg = if selected {
349:             if focused {
350:                 theme::SELECT
351:             } else {
352:                 theme::PANEL_ALT
353:             }
354:         } else {
355:             theme::PANEL
356:         };

358:         let label = format!("{label:>2} ");
359:         let color = status_color(&label);
360:         let path = clip_display(&change.path, width.saturating_sub(3));
361:         let path_width = display_width(&path);
362: 
363:         let mut spans = vec![
364:             Span::styled(label, Style::default().fg(color).bg(bg)),
365:             Span::styled(path, Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(bg)),
366:         ];
367:         let used = 3 + path_width;
368:         if used < width {
369:             spans.push(Span::styled(
370:                 " ".repeat(width - used),
371:                 Style::default().bg(bg),
372:             ));
373:         }
374:         lines.push(Line::from(spans));
375:     }
376: 
377:     frame.render_widget(
378:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
379:         body,
380:     );
381: }

383: fn draw_git_input(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
384:     let focused = app.focus == Focus::Tree && app.git.input_focus;
385:     let prompt = if focused {
386:         Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT)
387:     } else {
388:         Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT)
389:     };
390:     let mut spans = vec![Span::styled("> ", prompt)];
391:     let width = area.width as usize;
392:     let available = width.saturating_sub(2);
393: 
394:     if app.git.input.is_empty() {
395:         spans.push(Span::styled(
396:             "commit message",
397:             Style::default()
398:                 .fg(theme::FAINT)
399:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
400:         ));
401:     } else {
402:         let chars: Vec<char> = app.git.input.chars().collect();
403:         let prefix: String = chars[..app.git.input_cursor.min(chars.len())]
404:             .iter()
405:             .collect();
406:         let prefix_width = display_width(&prefix);
407:         let start = prefix_width.saturating_sub(available.saturating_sub(1));
408:         let visible = clip_from(&app.git.input, start, available);
409:         spans.push(Span::styled(visible, Style::default().fg(theme::FG)));
410:         if focused {
411:             let x = area.x as usize + 2 + prefix_width.saturating_sub(start);
412:             if x < (area.x + area.width) as usize {
413:                 frame.set_cursor_position(Position::new(x as u16, area.y));
414:             }
415:         }
416:     }
417: 
418:     if focused && app.git.input.is_empty() {
419:         frame.set_cursor_position(Position::new(area.x + 2, area.y));
420:     }
421: 
422:     frame.render_widget(
423:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
424:         area,
425:     );
426: }
427: 
428: fn draw_editor(frame: &mut Frame, rect: Rect, app: &mut App) {
429:     frame.render_widget(Block::default().style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)), rect);
430:     app.ui.editor = rect;
431: 
432:     let inner = Rect {
433:         x: rect.x + 1,
434:         y: rect.y,
435:         width: rect.width.saturating_sub(2),
436:         height: rect.height,
437:     };
438:     if inner.width == 0 || inner.height == 0 {
439:         return;
440:     }
441: 
442:     let header = Rect {
443:         x: inner.x,
444:         y: inner.y,
445:         width: inner.width,
446:         height: 1,
447:     };
448: 
449:     app.ui.tabs.clear();
450:     app.ui.unsupported_button = Rect::default();
451:     if app.diff.is_some() {
452:         draw_diff(frame, inner, app);
453:         return;
454:     }
455:     if let Some(path) = app.unsupported.clone() {
456:         draw_unsupported(frame, inner, header, app, &path);
457:         return;
458:     }
459: 
460:     if let Some(buffer) = app.editor.buffer() {
461:         let focused = app.focus == Focus::Editor;
462:         let style = if focused {
463:             Style::default().fg(theme::FG).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
464:         } else {
465:             Style::default().fg(theme::DIM)
466:         };
467:         let track = app.track_label_for(&buffer.path).unwrap_or_default();
468:         let right = if track.is_empty() {
469:             String::new()
470:         } else {
471:             format!("pi {track}")
472:         };
473:         let right_width = display_width(&right).min(inner.width as usize / 2) as u16;
474:         let [left_area, right_area] =
475:             Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Min(0), Constraint::Length(right_width)]).areas(header);
476: 
477:         let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(
478:             clip_display(&buffer.rel, left_area.width as usize),
479:             style,
480:         )];
481:         if buffer.dirty {
482:             spans.push(Span::styled(" *", Style::default().fg(theme::AMBER)));
483:         }
484:         if buffer.external_change {
485:             spans.push(Span::styled(
486:                 " !",
487:                 Style::default()
488:                     .fg(theme::AMBER)
489:                     .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
490:             ));
491:         }
492:         frame.render_widget(
493:             Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)).style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
494:             left_area,
495:         );
496:         frame.render_widget(
497:             Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
498:                 clip_display(&right, right_area.width as usize),
499:                 Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
500:             )))
501:             .alignment(Alignment::Right)
502:             .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
503:             right_area,
504:         );
505:     }
506: 
507:     let tabs_height = if app.editor.buffers.len() > 1 && inner.height > 2 {
508:         1
509:     } else {
510:         0
511:     };
512:     if tabs_height == 1 {
513:         let y = inner.y + 1;
514:         let right = inner.x.saturating_add(inner.width);
515:         let mut x = inner.x;
516:         let mut spans = Vec::new();
517:         for (index, buffer) in app.editor.buffers.iter().enumerate() {
518:             let name = buffer
519:                 .path
520:                 .file_name()
521:                 .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().to_string())
522:                 .unwrap_or_else(|| buffer.rel.clone());
523:             let label = format!(" {name} ");
524:             let width = display_width(&label) as u16;
525:             if width == 0 || x.saturating_add(width) > right {
526:                 break;
527:             }
528:             let rect = Rect {
529:                 x,
530:                 y,
531:                 width,
532:                 height: 1,
533:             };
534:             let hovered = app.hover.map_or(false, |(hx, hy)| {
535:                 hx >= rect.x && hx < rect.x + rect.width && hy == rect.y
536:             });
537:             let style = if index == app.editor.active {
538:                 Style::default()
539:                     .fg(theme::ACCENT)
540:                     .bg(theme::SELECT)
541:                     .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
542:             } else if hovered {
543:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(theme::HOVER)
544:             } else {
545:                 Style::default().fg(theme::DIM)
546:             };
547:             spans.push(Span::styled(label, style));
548:             app.ui.tabs.push((rect, index));
549:             x = x.saturating_add(width);
550:         }
551:         frame.render_widget(
552:             Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)).style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
553:             Rect {
554:                 x: inner.x,
555:                 y,
556:                 width: inner.width,
557:                 height: 1,
558:             },
559:         );
560:     }
561: 
562:     let text_area = Rect {
563:         x: inner.x,
564:         y: inner.y + 1 + tabs_height,
565:         width: inner.width,
566:         height: inner.height.saturating_sub(1 + tabs_height),
567:     };
568:     app.ui.editor_text = text_area;
569: 
570:     let Some(line_count) = app.editor.buffer().map(|buffer| buffer.lines.len()) else {
571:         app.ui.gutter = 0;
572:         frame.render_widget(
573:             Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
574:                 "select a file",
575:                 Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
576:             )))
577:             .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
578:             text_area,
579:         );
580:         return;
581:     };
582: 
583:     if text_area.height == 0 {
584:         return;
585:     }
586:     let digits = line_count.max(1).to_string().len();
587:     let gutter = (digits + 1) as u16;
588:     app.ui.gutter = gutter;
589:     let text_width = text_area.width.saturating_sub(gutter) as usize;
590:     if text_width == 0 {
591:         return;
592:     }
593: 
594:     let focused = app.focus == Focus::Editor;
595:     if app.follow_editor_cursor {
596:         app.editor
597:             .ensure_visible(text_area.height as usize, text_width);
598:     }
599:     let rows = app.editor.visual_rows(text_width);
600:     let wrap = app.editor.wrap;
601: 
602:     let tracks = &app.tracks;
603:     let Some(buffer) = app.editor.buffer() else {
604:         return;
605:     };
606:     let cursor = app.editor.cursor_visual(text_width);
607:     let ghost = app.editor.ghost.as_deref();
608:     let ghost_lines: Vec<&str> = ghost
609:         .map(|text| text.split('\n').collect())
610:         .unwrap_or_default();
611:     let height = text_area.height as usize;
612: 
613:     let mut lines = Vec::with_capacity(height);
614:     let mut out_row = 0usize;
615:     let mut display_row = buffer.scroll;
616:     while out_row < height {
617:         let Some(visual) = rows.get(display_row).copied() else {
618:             break;
619:         };
620:         let index = visual.line;
621:         let number = index + 1;
622:         let tracked = track_at(tracks, &buffer.path, number);
623:         let bg = match tracked {
624:             Some(TrackKind::Read) => theme::READ_BG,
625:             Some(TrackKind::Write) => theme::WRITE_BG,
626:             None if index == buffer.cursor_line => theme::CURSOR_LINE,
627:             None => theme::BG,
628:         };
629:         let number_color = match tracked {
630:             Some(TrackKind::Read) => theme::ACCENT,
631:             Some(TrackKind::Write) => theme::AMBER,
632:             None => theme::FAINT,
633:         };
634:         let label = if wrap && visual.start > 0 {
635:             " ".repeat(digits + 1)
636:         } else {
637:             format!("{:>width$} ", number, width = digits)
638:         };
639: 
640:         let mut plain_spans = Vec::new();
641:         let raw = if let Some(spans) = buffer.matching_spans(index) {
642:             spans
643:         } else {
644:             if !buffer.lines[index].is_empty() {
645:                 plain_spans.push((Style::default().fg(theme::FG), buffer.lines[index].clone()));
646:             }
647:             plain_spans.as_slice()
648:         };
649:         let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(
650:             label,
651:             Style::default().fg(number_color).bg(bg),
652:         )];
653:         let selection = selection_columns(buffer, index);
654:         let clipped = if wrap {
655:             wrapped_spans(raw, visual.start, visual.end, text_width, bg, selection)
656:         } else {
657:             clipped_spans(raw, buffer.hscroll, text_width, bg, selection)
658:         };
659:         let mut used: usize = clipped
660:             .iter()
661:             .map(|span| display_width(&span.content))
662:             .sum();
663:         spans.extend(clipped);
664: 
665:         let cursor_row = focused
666:             && cursor.map(|(cursor_row, _)| cursor_row == display_row) == Some(true)
667:             && buffer.selection.is_none();
668:         if cursor_row {
669:             if let (Some(first), Some((_, column))) = (ghost_lines.first(), cursor) {
670:                 let start = if wrap {
671:                     column
672:                 } else {
673:                     column.saturating_sub(buffer.hscroll)
674:                 };
675:                 if start < text_width {
676:                     if used < start {
677:                         spans.push(Span::styled(
678:                             " ".repeat(start - used),
679:                             Style::default().bg(bg),
680:                         ));
681:                         used = start;
682:                     }
683:                     let visible = clip_display(first, text_width.saturating_sub(start));
684:                     used += display_width(&visible);
685:                     spans.push(Span::styled(
686:                         visible,
687:                         Style::default()
688:                             .fg(theme::DIM)
689:                             .bg(bg)
690:                             .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
691:                     ));
692:                 }
693:             }
694:         }
695: 
696:         if used < text_width {
697:             spans.push(Span::styled(
698:                 " ".repeat(text_width - used),
699:                 Style::default().bg(bg),
700:             ));
701:         }
702:         lines.push(Line::from(spans));
703:         out_row += 1;
704: 
705:         if cursor_row && ghost_lines.len() > 1 {
706:             for continuation in ghost_lines.iter().skip(1) {
707:                 if out_row >= height {
708:                     break;
709:                 }
710:                 let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(
711:                     " ".repeat(digits + 1),
712:                     Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT).bg(theme::BG),
713:                 )];
714:                 let visible = clip_display(continuation, text_width);
715:                 let used = display_width(&visible);
716:                 spans.push(Span::styled(
717:                     visible,
718:                     Style::default()
719:                         .fg(theme::DIM)
720:                         .bg(theme::BG)
721:                         .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
722:                 ));
723:                 if used < text_width {
724:                     spans.push(Span::styled(
725:                         " ".repeat(text_width - used),
726:                         Style::default().bg(theme::BG),
727:                     ));
728:                 }
729:                 lines.push(Line::from(spans));
730:                 out_row += 1;
731:             }
732:         }
733: 
734:         display_row += 1;
735:     }
736: 
737:     frame.render_widget(
738:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
739:         text_area,
740:     );
741: 
742:     if focused {
743:         if let Some((row, column)) = cursor {
744:             if row >= buffer.scroll && row < buffer.scroll + height {
745:                 let column = if wrap {
746:                     column
747:                 } else {
748:                     column.saturating_sub(buffer.hscroll)
749:                 };
750:                 let x = text_area.x as usize + gutter as usize + column;
751:                 let y = text_area.y + (row - buffer.scroll) as u16;
752:                 if x < (text_area.x + text_area.width) as usize {
753:                     frame.set_cursor_position(Position::new(x as u16, y));
754:                 }
755:             }
756:         }
757:     }
758: }
759: 
760: fn draw_unsupported(frame: &mut Frame, inner: Rect, header: Rect, app: &mut App, path: &Path) {
761:     app.ui.editor_text = Rect::default();
762:     app.ui.gutter = 0;
763:     let rel = app.editor.rel_of(path);
764:     frame.render_widget(
765:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
766:             clip_display(&rel, header.width as usize),
767:             Style::default().fg(theme::FG).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
768:         )))
769:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
770:         header,
771:     );
772: 
773:     let body = Rect {
774:         x: inner.x,
775:         y: inner.y + 1,
776:         width: inner.width,
777:         height: inner.height.saturating_sub(1),
778:     };
779:     if body.width == 0 || body.height < 4 {
780:         return;
781:     }
782: 
783:     let message_y = body.y + body.height / 2 - 1;
784:     frame.render_widget(
785:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
786:             "this file type is not supported.",
787:             Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
788:         )))
789:         .alignment(Alignment::Center)
790:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
791:         Rect {
792:             x: body.x,
793:             y: message_y,
794:             width: body.width,
795:             height: 1,
796:         },
797:     );
798: 
799:     let label = " open in default app ";
800:     let width = display_width(label) as u16;
801:     if width > body.width {
802:         return;
803:     }
804:     let button = Rect {
805:         x: body.x + (body.width - width) / 2,
806:         y: message_y + 2,
807:         width,
808:         height: 1,
809:     };
810:     if button.y >= body.y + body.height {
811:         return;
812:     }
813:     app.ui.unsupported_button = button;
814:     let hovered = app.hover.map_or(false, |(x, y)| {
815:         x >= button.x
816:             && x < button.x + button.width
817:             && y >= button.y
818:             && y < button.y + button.height
819:     });
820:     let style = if hovered {
821:         Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(theme::SELECT)
822:     } else {
823:         Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT).bg(theme::PANEL_ALT)
824:     };
825:     frame.render_widget(
826:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(label, style)))
827:             .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
828:         button,
829:     );
830: }
831: 
832: fn working_label(elapsed_ms: u128) -> &'static str {
833:     const FRAMES: [&str; 4] = ["working", "working.", "working..", "working..."];
834:     FRAMES[(elapsed_ms / 280) as usize % FRAMES.len()]
835: }
836: 
837: fn draw_agent(frame: &mut Frame, rect: Rect, app: &mut App) {
838:     let border = if matches!(app.resizing, Some(ResizeEdge::Agent)) {
839:         theme::ACCENT
840:     } else if app.hover_edge == Some(ResizeEdge::Agent) {
841:         theme::DIM
842:     } else {
843:         theme::BORDER
844:     };
845:     let block = Block::default()
846:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL))
847:         .borders(Borders::LEFT)
848:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(border));
849:     let inner = block.inner(rect);
850:     frame.render_widget(block, rect);
851: 
852:     let content = Rect {
853:         x: inner.x + 1,
854:         y: inner.y,
855:         width: inner.width.saturating_sub(2),
856:         height: inner.height,
857:     };
858:     app.ui.agent = content;
859:     app.ui.agent_input = Rect::default();
860:     if content.width == 0 || content.height == 0 {
861:         return;
862:     }
863: 
864:     let (status, status_color) = match &app.pi {
865:         PiState::Missing => ("not installed", theme::AMBER),
866:         PiState::Failed(_) => ("failed", theme::RED),
867:         PiState::Running(_) if app.agent.busy => (
868:             working_label(app.now.duration_since(app.started_at).as_millis()),
869:             theme::ACCENT,
870:         ),
871:         PiState::Running(_) => (app.model.as_deref().unwrap_or("idle"), theme::DIM),
872:     };
873:     let title_style = if app.focus == Focus::Agent {
874:         Style::default().fg(theme::FG).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
875:     } else {
876:         Style::default()
877:             .fg(theme::FAINT)
878:             .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
879:     };
880:     let status_width = display_width(status).min(content.width as usize / 2) as u16;
881:     let [title_area, status_area] =
882:         Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Min(0), Constraint::Length(status_width)]).areas(Rect {
883:             x: content.x,
884:             y: content.y,
885:             width: content.width,
886:             height: 1,
887:         });
888:     frame.render_widget(
889:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled("agent", title_style)))
890:             .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
891:         title_area,
892:     );
893:     frame.render_widget(
894:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
895:             clip_display(status, status_area.width as usize),
896:             Style::default().fg(status_color),
897:         )))
898:         .alignment(Alignment::Right)
899:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
900:         status_area,
901:     );
902: 
903:     let running = matches!(app.pi, PiState::Running(_));
904:     let input_height = if running && content.height >= 2 { 1 } else { 0 };
905:     let body = Rect {
906:         x: content.x,
907:         y: content.y + 1,
908:         width: content.width,
909:         height: content.height.saturating_sub(1 + input_height),
910:     };
911: 
912:     app.ui.agent_thinking.clear();
913:     match &app.pi {
914:         PiState::Missing if app.agent.entries.is_empty() => draw_missing(frame, body),
915:         PiState::Failed(error) if app.agent.entries.is_empty() => draw_failed(frame, body, error),
916:         _ => draw_conversation(frame, body, app),
917:     }
918: 
919:     if input_height == 1 {
920:         let input = Rect {
921:             x: content.x,
922:             y: content.y + content.height - 1,
923:             width: content.width,
924:             height: 1,
925:         };
926:         app.ui.agent_input = input;
927:         draw_agent_input(frame, input, app);
928:     }
929: }
930: 
931: fn draw_missing(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
932:     if area.height == 0 || area.width == 0 {
933:         return;
934:     }
935:     let width = area.width as usize;
936:     let mut lines = vec![
937:         Line::from(Span::styled(
938:             "pi not installed",
939:             Style::default()
940:                 .fg(theme::AMBER)
941:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
942:         )),
943:         Line::from(""),
944:         Line::from(Span::styled(
945:             "https://pi.dev",
946:             Style::default()
947:                 .fg(theme::ACCENT)
948:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::UNDERLINED),
949:         )),
950:         Line::from(""),
951:     ];
952:     for part in wrap_text(install_command(), width) {
953:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
954:             part,
955:             Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
956:         )));
957:     }
958:     lines.push(Line::from(""));
959:     for part in wrap_text("restart smol after installing", width) {
960:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
961:             part,
962:             Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
963:         )));
964:     }
965:     frame.render_widget(
966:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
967:         area,
968:     );
969: }
970: 
971: fn draw_failed(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, error: &str) {
972:     if area.height == 0 || area.width == 0 {
973:         return;
974:     }
975:     let width = area.width as usize;
976:     let mut lines = vec![
977:         Line::from(Span::styled(
978:             "pi failed",
979:             Style::default().fg(theme::RED).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
980:         )),
981:         Line::from(""),
982:     ];
983:     for part in wrap_text(error, width) {
984:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
985:             part,
986:             Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
987:         )));
988:     }
989:     lines.push(Line::from(""));
990:     lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
991:         "https://pi.dev",
992:         Style::default()
993:             .fg(theme::ACCENT)
994:             .add_modifier(Modifier::UNDERLINED),
995:     )));
996:     frame.render_widget(
997:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
998:         area,
999:     );
1000: }
1001: 
1002: fn draw_conversation(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1003:     if area.height == 0 || area.width == 0 {
1004:         return;
1005:     }
1006:     let width = area.width as usize;
1007:     let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> = Vec::new();
1008:     let mut thinking_rows: Vec<(usize, usize)> = Vec::new();
1009: 
1010:     for (entry_index, entry) in app.agent.entries.iter().enumerate() {
1011:         match entry {
1012:             Entry::User(text) => {
1013:                 if !text.trim().is_empty() {
1014:                     if !lines.is_empty() {
1015:                         lines.push(Line::from(""));
1016:                     }
1017:                     lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1018:                         "you",
1019:                         Style::default()
1020:                             .fg(theme::ACCENT)
1021:                             .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
1022:                     )));
1023:                     lines.extend(render_prompt(text, width));
1024:                 }
1025:             }
1026:             Entry::Assistant {
1027:                 text,
1028:                 thinking,
1029:                 thinking_open,
1030:             } => {
1031:                 let has_thinking = !thinking.trim().is_empty();
1032:                 let has_text = !text.trim().is_empty();
1033:                 if has_thinking || has_text {
1034:                     if !lines.is_empty() {
1035:                         lines.push(Line::from(""));
1036:                     }
1037:                     if has_thinking {
1038:                         thinking_rows.push((lines.len(), entry_index));
1039:                         let marker = if *thinking_open {
1040:                             "\u{25be}"
1041:                         } else {
1042:                             "\u{25b8}"
1043:                         };
1044:                         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1045:                             format!("{marker} thinking"),
1046:                             Style::default()
1047:                                 .fg(theme::DIM)
1048:                                 .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
1049:                         )));
1050:                         if *thinking_open {
1051:                             for part in wrap_text(thinking.trim_end(), width) {
1052:                                 lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1053:                                     part,
1054:                                     Style::default()
1055:                                         .fg(theme::FAINT)
1056:                                         .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
1057:                                 )));
1058:                             }
1059:                         }
1060:                         if has_text {
1061:                             lines.push(Line::from(""));
1062:                         }
1063:                     }
1064:                     if has_text {
1065:                         lines.extend(crate::markdown::render(text, width));
1066:                     }
1067:                 }
1068:             }
1069:             Entry::Tool(tool) => {
1070:                 let color = match tool.state {
1071:                     ToolState::Running => theme::ACCENT,
1072:                     ToolState::Done => theme::DIM,
1073:                     ToolState::Failed => theme::RED,
1074:                 };
1075:                 let mut label = tool.label.clone();
1076:                 if tool.state == ToolState::Running {
1077:                     let frames = [".  ", ".. ", "...", " .."];
1078:                     let frame = (app.now.duration_since(app.started_at).as_millis() / 180) as usize
1079:                         % frames.len();
1080:                     label.push(' ');
1081:                     label.push_str(frames[frame]);
1082:                 }
1083:                 for part in wrap_text(&label, width) {
1084:                     lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(part, Style::default().fg(color))));
1085:                 }
1086:                 if !tool.body.is_empty() && width > 2 {
1087:                     for body in &tool.body {
1088:                         let body_color = match body.kind {
1089:                             ToolBodyKind::Text => theme::FG,
1090:                             ToolBodyKind::Add => theme::GREEN,
1091:                             ToolBodyKind::Del => theme::RED,
1092:                             ToolBodyKind::Meta => theme::FAINT,
1093:                         };
1094:                         for part in wrap_text(&body.text, width - 2) {
1095:                             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1096:                                 format!("  {part}"),
1097:                                 Style::default().fg(body_color),
1098:                             )));
1099:                         }
1100:                     }
1101:                 }
1102:             }
1103:             Entry::Note(note) => {
1104:                 for part in wrap_text(note, width) {
1105:                     lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1106:                         part,
1107:                         Style::default().fg(theme::AMBER),
1108:                     )));
1109:                 }
1110:             }
1111:         }
1112:     }
1113: 
1114:     if lines.is_empty() {
1115:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1116:             "ask pi about this project",
1117:             Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1118:         )));
1119:     }
1120: 
1121:     let height = area.height as usize;
1122:     let max_scroll = lines.len().saturating_sub(height);
1123:     app.agent.scroll = app.agent.scroll.min(max_scroll);
1124:     let start = lines
1125:         .len()
1126:         .saturating_sub(height)
1127:         .saturating_sub(app.agent.scroll);
1128:     let end = (start + height).min(lines.len());
1129:     let visible: Vec<Line<'static>> = lines[start..end].to_vec();
1130:     for (row, entry_index) in thinking_rows {
1131:         if row >= start && row < end {
1132:             app.ui.agent_thinking.push((
1133:                 Rect {
1134:                     x: area.x,
1135:                     y: area.y + (row - start) as u16,
1136:                     width: area.width,
1137:                     height: 1,
1138:                 },
1139:                 entry_index,
1140:             ));
1141:         }
1142:     }
1143: 
1144:     frame.render_widget(
1145:         Paragraph::new(visible).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1146:         area,
1147:     );
1148: }
1149: 
1150: fn render_prompt(text: &str, width: usize) -> Vec<Line<'static>> {
1151:     let source: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
1152:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1153:     let mut plain_start = 0;
1154:     let mut index = 0;
1155: 
1156:     while index < source.len() {
1157:         let Some(name) = opening_tag(source[index]) else {
1158:             index += 1;
1159:             continue;
1160:         };
1161:         let Some(close) = (index + 1..source.len())
1162:             .find(|candidate| closing_tag(source[*candidate]) == Some(name))
1163:         else {
1164:             index += 1;
1165:             continue;
1166:         };
1167: 
1168:         push_prompt_section(
1169:             &mut lines,
1170:             crate::markdown::render(&source[plain_start..index].join("\n"), width),
1171:         );
1172:         let mut block = vec![Line::from(Span::styled(
1173:             name.to_string(),
1174:             Style::default()
1175:                 .fg(theme::DIM)
1176:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
1177:         ))];
1178:         block.extend(crate::markdown::code_block(
1179:             &source[index + 1..close].join("\n"),
1180:             width,
1181:         ));
1182:         push_prompt_section(&mut lines, block);
1183: 
1184:         index = close + 1;
1185:         plain_start = index;
1186:     }
1187: 
1188:     if plain_start < source.len() {
1189:         push_prompt_section(
1190:             &mut lines,
1191:             crate::markdown::render(&source[plain_start..].join("\n"), width),
1192:         );
1193:     }
1194: 
1195:     lines
1196: }
1197: 
1198: fn push_prompt_section(lines: &mut Vec<Line<'static>>, section: Vec<Line<'static>>) {
1199:     if section
1200:         .iter()
1201:         .all(|line| line.spans.iter().all(|span| span.content.trim().is_empty()))
1202:     {
1203:         return;
1204:     }
1205:     if !lines.is_empty() {
1206:         lines.push(Line::from(""));
1207:     }
1208:     lines.extend(section);
1209: }
1210: 
1211: fn opening_tag(line: &str) -> Option<&str> {
1212:     let inner = line.trim().strip_prefix('<')?.strip_suffix('>')?;
1213:     let name = inner.split_whitespace().next()?;
1214:     valid_tag_name(name).then_some(name)
1215: }
1216: 
1217: fn closing_tag(line: &str) -> Option<&str> {
1218:     let inner = line.trim().strip_prefix("</")?.strip_suffix('>')?.trim();
1219:     valid_tag_name(inner).then_some(inner)
1220: }
1221: 
1222: fn valid_tag_name(name: &str) -> bool {
1223:     !name.is_empty()
1224:         && name
1225:             .chars()
1226:             .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(ch, '_' | '-'))
1227: }
1228: 
1229: fn draw_agent_input(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1230:     let focused = app.focus == Focus::Agent;
1231:     let hovered = app.hover.map_or(false, |(x, y)| {
1232:         x >= area.x && x < area.x + area.width && y >= area.y && y < area.y + area.height
1233:     });
1234:     let prompt_style = if focused || hovered {
1235:         Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT)
1236:     } else {
1237:         Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT)
1238:     };
1239:     let mut spans = vec![Span::styled("> ", prompt_style)];
1240:     let width = area.width as usize;
1241:     let available = width.saturating_sub(2);
1242: 
1243:     if app.agent.input.is_empty() {
1244:         spans.push(Span::styled(
1245:             "ask pi",
1246:             Style::default()
1247:                 .fg(theme::FAINT)
1248:                 .add_modifier(Modifier::ITALIC),
1249:         ));
1250:     } else {
1251:         let chars: Vec<char> = app.agent.input.chars().collect();
1252:         let prefix: String = chars[..app.agent.input_cursor.min(chars.len())]
1253:             .iter()
1254:             .collect();
1255:         let prefix_width = display_width(&prefix);
1256:         let start = prefix_width.saturating_sub(available.saturating_sub(1));
1257:         let visible = clip_from(&app.agent.input, start, available);
1258:         spans.push(Span::styled(visible, Style::default().fg(theme::FG)));
1259:         if focused {
1260:             let x = area.x as usize + 2 + prefix_width.saturating_sub(start);
1261:             if x < (area.x + area.width) as usize {
1262:                 frame.set_cursor_position(Position::new(x as u16, area.y));
1263:             }
1264:         }
1265:     }
1266: 
1267:     if focused && app.agent.input.is_empty() {
1268:         frame.set_cursor_position(Position::new(area.x + 2, area.y));
1269:     }
1270: 
1271:     frame.render_widget(
1272:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1273:         area,
1274:     );
1275: 
1276:     if focused && !app.agent.suggestions.is_empty() {
1277:         draw_agent_suggestions(frame, area, app);
1278:     }
1279: }
1280: 
1281: fn draw_agent_suggestions(frame: &mut Frame, input: Rect, app: &App) {
1282:     let top = app.ui.agent.y.saturating_add(1);
1283:     let available = input.y.saturating_sub(top) as usize;
1284:     if input.width < 4 || available < 3 {
1285:         return;
1286:     }
1287:     let rows = app
1288:         .agent
1289:         .suggestions
1290:         .len()
1291:         .min(8)
1292:         .min(available.saturating_sub(2));
1293:     if rows == 0 {
1294:         return;
1295:     }
1296: 
1297:     let height = rows as u16 + 2;
1298:     let popup = Rect {
1299:         x: input.x,
1300:         y: input.y.saturating_sub(height),
1301:         width: input.width,
1302:         height,
1303:     };
1304:     frame.render_widget(Clear, popup);
1305: 
1306:     let block = Block::default()
1307:         .title(" files ")
1308:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
1309:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT))
1310:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL));
1311:     let inner = block.inner(popup);
1312:     let start = app
1313:         .agent
1314:         .suggestion_selected
1315:         .saturating_sub(rows.saturating_sub(1));
1316:     let lines = app
1317:         .agent
1318:         .suggestions
1319:         .iter()
1320:         .skip(start)
1321:         .take(rows)
1322:         .enumerate()
1323:         .map(|(offset, path)| {
1324:             let selected = start + offset == app.agent.suggestion_selected;
1325:             let style = if selected {
1326:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(theme::SELECT)
1327:             } else {
1328:                 Style::default().fg(theme::DIM).bg(theme::PANEL)
1329:             };
1330:             Line::from(Span::styled(format!(" @{path}"), style))
1331:         })
1332:         .collect::<Vec<_>>();
1333: 
1334:     frame.render_widget(block, popup);
1335:     frame.render_widget(
1336:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1337:         inner,
1338:     );
1339: }
1340: 
1341: fn draw_diff(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1342:     app.ui.gutter = 0;
1343:     app.ui.editor_text = Rect {
1344:         x: area.x,
1345:         y: area.y + 1,
1346:         width: area.width,
1347:         height: area.height.saturating_sub(1),
1348:     };
1349: 
1350:     let Some(diff) = &mut app.diff else {
1351:         return;
1352:     };
1353:     let header = Rect {
1354:         x: area.x,
1355:         y: area.y,
1356:         width: area.width,
1357:         height: 1,
1358:     };
1359:     frame.render_widget(
1360:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(vec![
1361:             Span::styled(
1362:                 "diff",
1363:                 Style::default()
1364:                     .fg(theme::ACCENT)
1365:                     .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
1366:             ),
1367:             Span::styled("  ", Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT)),
1368:             Span::styled(
1369:                 clip_display(&diff.title, area.width.saturating_sub(6) as usize),
1370:                 Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
1371:             ),
1372:         ]))
1373:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
1374:         header,
1375:     );
1376: 
1377:     let body = app.ui.editor_text;
1378:     if body.width == 0 || body.height == 0 {
1379:         return;
1380:     }
1381:     let height = body.height as usize;
1382:     let max_scroll = diff.lines.len().saturating_sub(height);
1383:     diff.scroll = diff.scroll.min(max_scroll);
1384:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1385:     for line in diff.lines.iter().skip(diff.scroll).take(height) {
1386:         let color = match line.kind {
1387:             DiffKind::Add => theme::GREEN,
1388:             DiffKind::Del => theme::RED,
1389:             DiffKind::Hunk => theme::ACCENT,
1390:             DiffKind::Meta => theme::DIM,
1391:             DiffKind::Context => theme::FG,
1392:         };
1393:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1394:             clip_display(&line.text, body.width as usize),
1395:             Style::default().fg(color),
1396:         )));
1397:     }
1398:     if lines.is_empty() {
1399:         lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1400:             "no diff",
1401:             Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1402:         )));
1403:     }
1404:     frame.render_widget(
1405:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::BG)),
1406:         body,
1407:     );
1408: }
1409: 
1410: fn draw_menu(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1411:     let Some(menu) = &app.menu else {
1412:         return;
1413:     };
1414:     let Some(page) = menu.pages.last() else {
1415:         return;
1416:     };
1417:     let width = area.width.saturating_sub(8).min(64).max(32).min(area.width);
1418:     let height = (page.items.len() as u16 + 2).min(area.height);
1419:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
1420:         return;
1421:     }
1422:     let rect = Rect {
1423:         x: area.x + (area.width - width) / 2,
1424:         y: area.y + (area.height - height) / 2,
1425:         width,
1426:         height,
1427:     };
1428:     frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
1429:     let title = if menu.pages.len() > 1 {
1430:         format!(" commands / {} ", page.title)
1431:     } else {
1432:         format!(" {} ", page.title)
1433:     };
1434:     let block = Block::default()
1435:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
1436:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT))
1437:         .title(title)
1438:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL));
1439:     let inner = block.inner(rect);
1440:     frame.render_widget(block, rect);
1441:     if inner.width == 0 || inner.height == 0 {
1442:         return;
1443:     }
1444: 
1445:     let width = inner.width as usize;
1446:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1447:     for (index, item) in page.items.iter().enumerate().take(inner.height as usize) {
1448:         let selected = index == page.selected;
1449:         let bg = if selected {
1450:             theme::SELECT
1451:         } else {
1452:             theme::PANEL
1453:         };
1454:         let label_style = Style::default()
1455:             .fg(if selected { theme::FG } else { theme::DIM })
1456:             .bg(bg);
1457:         let hint_style = Style::default()
1458:             .fg(if selected { theme::FG } else { theme::FAINT })
1459:             .bg(bg);
1460:         let hint = clip_display(&item.hint, width / 2);
1461:         let hint_width = display_width(&hint);
1462:         let label = clip_display(&item.label, width.saturating_sub(hint_width + 2));
1463:         let label_width = display_width(&label);
1464:         let used = label_width + hint_width;
1465:         let gap = width.saturating_sub(used);
1466: 
1467:         let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(label, label_style)];
1468:         if !hint.is_empty() {
1469:             spans.push(Span::styled(" ".repeat(gap), label_style));
1470:             spans.push(Span::styled(hint, hint_style));
1471:         } else if gap > 0 {
1472:             spans.push(Span::styled(" ".repeat(gap), label_style));
1473:         }
1474:         lines.push(Line::from(spans));
1475:     }
1476:     frame.render_widget(
1477:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1478:         inner,
1479:     );
1480: }
1481: 
1482: fn draw_context_menu(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1483:     app.ui.context_menu = Rect::default();
1484:     let Some(menu) = &app.context_menu else {
1485:         return;
1486:     };
1487:     let label_width = menu
1488:         .items
1489:         .iter()
1490:         .map(|item| display_width(item.label))
1491:         .max()
1492:         .unwrap_or(0);
1493:     let hint_width = menu
1494:         .items
1495:         .iter()
1496:         .map(|item| display_width(item.hint))
1497:         .max()
1498:         .unwrap_or(0);
1499:     let width = (label_width + hint_width + 4).clamp(24, 48) as u16;
1500:     let width = width.min(area.width);
1501:     let height = (menu.items.len() as u16 + 2).min(area.height);
1502:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
1503:         return;
1504:     }
1505:     let max_x = area.x.saturating_add(area.width.saturating_sub(width));
1506:     let max_y = area.y.saturating_add(area.height.saturating_sub(height));
1507:     let rect = Rect {
1508:         x: menu.anchor.0.min(max_x).max(area.x),
1509:         y: menu.anchor.1.min(max_y).max(area.y),
1510:         width,
1511:         height,
1512:     };
1513:     app.ui.context_menu = rect;
1514:     frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
1515: 
1516:     let name = menu
1517:         .target
1518:         .file_name()
1519:         .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1520:         .unwrap_or_else(|| menu.target.display().to_string());
1521:     let title = format!(
1522:         " {} ",
1523:         clip_display(&name, width.saturating_sub(4) as usize)
1524:     );
1525:     let block = Block::default()
1526:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
1527:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(theme::ACCENT))
1528:         .title(title)
1529:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL));
1530:     let inner = block.inner(rect);
1531:     frame.render_widget(block, rect);
1532:     if inner.width == 0 || inner.height == 0 {
1533:         return;
1534:     }
1535: 
1536:     let width = inner.width as usize;
1537:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1538:     for (index, item) in menu.items.iter().enumerate().take(inner.height as usize) {
1539:         let selected = index == menu.selected;
1540:         let bg = if selected {
1541:             theme::SELECT
1542:         } else {
1543:             theme::PANEL
1544:         };
1545:         let label_style = Style::default()
1546:             .fg(if selected { theme::FG } else { theme::DIM })
1547:             .bg(bg);
1548:         let hint_style = Style::default()
1549:             .fg(if selected { theme::FG } else { theme::FAINT })
1550:             .bg(bg);
1551:         let hint = clip_display(item.hint, width / 2);
1552:         let hint_width = display_width(&hint);
1553:         let label = clip_display(item.label, width.saturating_sub(hint_width + 2));
1554:         let label_width = display_width(&label);
1555:         let gap = width.saturating_sub(label_width + hint_width);
1556:         let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(label, label_style)];
1557:         if !hint.is_empty() {
1558:             spans.push(Span::styled(" ".repeat(gap), label_style));
1559:             spans.push(Span::styled(hint, hint_style));
1560:         } else if gap > 0 {
1561:             spans.push(Span::styled(" ".repeat(gap), label_style));
1562:         }
1563:         lines.push(Line::from(spans));
1564:     }
1565:     frame.render_widget(
1566:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1567:         inner,
1568:     );
1569: }
1570: 
1571: fn draw_file_dialog(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1572:     let Some(dialog) = &app.dialog else {
1573:         return;
1574:     };
1575:     let width = area.width.saturating_sub(8).min(64).max(36).min(area.width);
1576:     let height = match dialog {
1577:         FileDialog::Delete { .. } => 8,
1578:         FileDialog::Rename { .. } | FileDialog::Create { .. } => 7,
1579:     }
1580:     .min(area.height);
1581:     if width == 0 || height == 0 {
1582:         return;
1583:     }
1584:     let rect = Rect {
1585:         x: area.x + (area.width - width) / 2,
1586:         y: area.y + (area.height - height) / 2,
1587:         width,
1588:         height,
1589:     };
1590:     frame.render_widget(Clear, rect);
1591: 
1592:     let (title, border) = match dialog {
1593:         FileDialog::Delete { .. } => (" delete ".to_string(), theme::RED),
1594:         FileDialog::Rename { prompt, .. } | FileDialog::Create { prompt, .. } => {
1595:             (format!(" {} ", prompt.title), theme::ACCENT)
1596:         }
1597:     };
1598:     let block = Block::default()
1599:         .borders(Borders::ALL)
1600:         .border_style(Style::default().fg(border))
1601:         .title(clip_display(&title, width.saturating_sub(4) as usize))
1602:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL));
1603:     let inner = block.inner(rect);
1604:     frame.render_widget(block, rect);
1605:     if inner.width == 0 || inner.height == 0 {
1606:         return;
1607:     }
1608: 
1609:     let width = inner.width as usize;
1610:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1611:     let mut cursor = None;
1612:     match dialog {
1613:         FileDialog::Rename { prompt, .. } | FileDialog::Create { prompt, .. } => {
1614:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1615:                 "name",
1616:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1617:             )));
1618:             lines.push(Line::default());
1619:             let shown = clip_display(&prompt.value, width);
1620:             let shown_width = display_width(&shown);
1621:             let mut spans = vec![Span::styled(
1622:                 shown,
1623:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FG).bg(theme::SELECT),
1624:             )];
1625:             if shown_width < width {
1626:                 spans.push(Span::styled(
1627:                     " ".repeat(width - shown_width),
1628:                     Style::default().bg(theme::SELECT),
1629:                 ));
1630:             }
1631:             lines.push(Line::from(spans));
1632:             lines.push(Line::default());
1633:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1634:                 "enter confirm  esc cancel",
1635:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1636:             )));
1637:             let before: String = prompt.value.chars().take(prompt.cursor).collect();
1638:             let x = inner.x + (display_width(&before) as u16).min(inner.width.saturating_sub(1));
1639:             cursor = Some(Position::new(x, inner.y + 2));
1640:         }
1641:         FileDialog::Delete {
1642:             target,
1643:             is_dir,
1644:             dirty,
1645:         } => {
1646:             let name = target
1647:                 .file_name()
1648:                 .map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().to_string())
1649:                 .unwrap_or_else(|| target.display().to_string());
1650:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1651:                 format!("Delete {name}?"),
1652:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FG),
1653:             )));
1654:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1655:                 if *is_dir {
1656:                     "The folder and all its contents will be removed."
1657:                 } else {
1658:                     "The file will be removed."
1659:                 },
1660:                 Style::default().fg(theme::RED),
1661:             )));
1662:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1663:                 if *dirty {
1664:                     "Unsaved changes will be lost."
1665:                 } else {
1666:                     "This cannot be undone."
1667:                 },
1668:                 Style::default().fg(theme::AMBER),
1669:             )));
1670:             lines.push(Line::default());
1671:             lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
1672:                 "enter delete  esc cancel",
1673:                 Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1674:             )));
1675:         }
1676:     }
1677:     frame.render_widget(
1678:         Paragraph::new(lines).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL)),
1679:         inner,
1680:     );
1681:     if let Some(position) = cursor {
1682:         frame.set_cursor_position(position);
1683:     }
1684: }
1685: 
1686: fn draw_status(frame: &mut Frame, area: Rect, app: &mut App) {
1687:     frame.render_widget(
1688:         Block::default().style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL_ALT)),
1689:         area,
1690:     );
1691: 
1692:     let hint = app.status_hint(area.width);
1693:     let max_hint = (area.width as usize * 3 / 4)
1694:         .max(24)
1695:         .min(area.width as usize);
1696:     let hint = clip_display(&hint, max_hint);
1697:     let hint_width = (display_width(&hint) as u16).min(area.width);
1698:     let [left, right] =
1699:         Layout::horizontal([Constraint::Min(0), Constraint::Length(hint_width)]).areas(area);
1700: 
1701:     let mut spans = Vec::new();
1702:     if let Some(buffer) = app.editor.buffer() {
1703:         spans.push(Span::styled(
1704:             clip_display(&buffer.rel, left.width as usize / 2),
1705:             Style::default().fg(theme::FG),
1706:         ));
1707:         spans.push(Span::styled(
1708:             format!("  {}:{}", buffer.cursor_line + 1, buffer.cursor_col + 1),
1709:             Style::default().fg(theme::DIM),
1710:         ));
1711:     } else {
1712:         spans.push(Span::styled(
1713:             app.tree
1714:                 .nodes
1715:                 .first()
1716:                 .map(|node| node.name.clone())
1717:                 .unwrap_or_default(),
1718:             Style::default().fg(theme::FG),
1719:         ));
1720:     }
1721:     if let Some((message, _)) = &app.status {
1722:         spans.push(Span::styled("  ", Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT)));
1723:         spans.push(Span::styled(
1724:             clip_display(message, left.width as usize / 2),
1725:             Style::default().fg(theme::AMBER),
1726:         ));
1727:     }
1728: 
1729:     frame.render_widget(
1730:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)).style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL_ALT)),
1731:         left,
1732:     );
1733:     frame.render_widget(
1734:         Paragraph::new(Line::from(Span::styled(
1735:             hint,
1736:             Style::default().fg(theme::FAINT),
1737:         )))
1738:         .alignment(Alignment::Right)
1739:         .style(Style::default().bg(theme::PANEL_ALT)),
1740:         right,
1741:     );
1742: }
1743: 
1744: fn track_at(tracks: &[Track], path: &Path, line: usize) -> Option<TrackKind> {
1745:     tracks
1746:         .iter()
1747:         .rev()
1748:         .find(|track| track.path == path && line >= track.start && line <= track.end)
1749:         .map(|track| track.kind)
1750: }
1751: 
1752: fn status_color(label: &str) -> Color {
1753:     if label.contains('?') || label.contains('A') {
1754:         theme::GREEN
1755:     } else if label.contains('U') || label.contains('D') {
1756:         theme::RED
1757:     } else {
1758:         theme::AMBER
1759:     }
1760: }
1761: 
1762: fn selection_columns(buffer: &Buffer, line: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
1763:     let anchor = buffer.selection?;
1764:     let cursor = (buffer.cursor_line, buffer.cursor_col);
1765:     let (start, end) = if anchor < cursor {
1766:         (anchor, cursor)
1767:     } else {
1768:         (cursor, anchor)
1769:     };
1770:     if line < start.0 || line > end.0 {
1771:         return None;
1772:     }
1773:     let length = buffer
1774:         .lines
1775:         .get(line)
1776:         .map(|line| line.chars().count())
1777:         .unwrap_or(0);
1778:     let start_col = if line == start.0 {
1779:         start.1.min(length)
1780:     } else {
1781:         0
1782:     };
1783:     let end_col = if line == end.0 {
1784:         end.1.min(length)
1785:     } else {
1786:         length
1787:     };
1788:     if start_col == end_col {
1789:         None
1790:     } else {
1791:         Some((start_col, end_col))
1792:     }
1793: }
1794: 
1795: fn wrapped_spans(
1796:     raw: &[(Style, String)],
1797:     start: usize,
1798:     end: usize,
1799:     width: usize,
1800:     bg: Color,
1801:     selection: Option<(usize, usize)>,
1802: ) -> Vec<Span<'static>> {
1803:     let mut pieces: Vec<(Style, String)> = Vec::new();
1804:     let mut index = 0usize;
1805:     let mut column = 0usize;
1806: 
1807:     'outer: for (style, text) in raw {
1808:         for ch in text.chars() {
1809:             if index >= end {
1810:                 break 'outer;
1811:             }
1812:             let selected = selection
1813:                 .map(|(start, end)| index >= start && index < end)
1814:                 .unwrap_or(false);
1815:             let piece_style = style.bg(if selected { theme::SELECTION } else { bg });
1816:             index += 1;
1817:             if index <= start {
1818:                 continue;
1819:             }
1820: 
1821:             if ch == '\t' {
1822:                 let next = (column / 4 + 1) * 4;
1823:                 while column < next && column < width {
1824:                     push_piece(piece_style, ' ', &mut pieces);
1825:                     column += 1;
1826:                 }
1827:                 continue;
1828:             }
1829:             let char_width = ch.width().unwrap_or(0);
1830:             if char_width == 0 {
1831:                 continue;
1832:             }
1833:             if column + char_width > width {
1834:                 break 'outer;
1835:             }
1836:             push_piece(piece_style, ch, &mut pieces);
1837:             column += char_width;
1838:         }
1839:     }
1840: 
1841:     pieces
1842:         .into_iter()
1843:         .map(|(style, text)| Span::styled(text, style))
1844:         .collect()
1845: }
1846: 
1847: fn clipped_spans(
1848:     raw: &[(Style, String)],
1849:     hscroll: usize,
1850:     width: usize,
1851:     bg: Color,
1852:     selection: Option<(usize, usize)>,
1853: ) -> Vec<Span<'static>> {
1854:     let mut pieces: Vec<(Style, String)> = Vec::new();
1855:     let mut column = 0usize;
1856:     let mut index = 0usize;
1857: 
1858:     'outer: for (style, text) in raw {
1859:         for ch in text.chars() {
1860:             let selected = selection
1861:                 .map(|(start, end)| index >= start && index < end)
1862:                 .unwrap_or(false);
1863:             let piece_style = style.bg(if selected { theme::SELECTION } else { bg });
1864:             index += 1;
1865: 
1866:             if ch == '\t' {
1867:                 let next = (column / 4 + 1) * 4;
1868:                 while column < next {
1869:                     if column >= hscroll && column - hscroll < width {
1870:                         push_piece(piece_style, ' ', &mut pieces);
1871:                     }
1872:                     column += 1;
1873:                 }
1874:                 continue;
1875:             }
1876:             let char_width = ch.width().unwrap_or(0);
1877:             if char_width == 0 {
1878:                 continue;
1879:             }
1880:             let start = column;
1881:             column += char_width;
1882:             if column > hscroll + width {
1883:                 break 'outer;
1884:             }
1885:             if start >= hscroll && start - hscroll + char_width <= width {
1886:                 push_piece(piece_style, ch, &mut pieces);
1887:             }
1888:         }
1889:     }
1890: 
1891:     pieces
1892:         .into_iter()
1893:         .map(|(style, text)| Span::styled(text, style))
1894:         .collect()
1895: }
1896: 
1897: fn push_piece(style: Style, ch: char, pieces: &mut Vec<(Style, String)>) {
1898:     if let Some((last_style, text)) = pieces.last_mut() {
1899:         if *last_style == style {
1900:             text.push(ch);
1901:             return;
1902:         }
1903:     }
1904:     pieces.push((style, ch.to_string()));
1905: }
1906: 
1907: fn clip_display(text: &str, width: usize) -> String {
1908:     if width == 0 {
1909:         return String::new();
1910:     }
1911:     let mut out = String::new();
1912:     let mut used = 0;
1913:     for ch in text.chars() {
1914:         let char_width = if ch == '\t' {
1915:             4
1916:         } else {
1917:             ch.width().unwrap_or(0)
1918:         };
1919:         if used + char_width > width {
1920:             break;
1921:         }
1922:         out.push(ch);
1923:         used += char_width;
1924:     }
1925:     out
1926: }
1927: 
1928: fn clip_from(text: &str, start: usize, width: usize) -> String {
1929:     if width == 0 {
1930:         return String::new();
1931:     }
1932:     let mut out = String::new();
1933:     let mut column = 0;
1934:     let mut used = 0;
1935:     for ch in text.chars() {
1936:         let char_width = if ch == '\t' {
1937:             4
1938:         } else {
1939:             ch.width().unwrap_or(0)
1940:         };
1941:         let next = column + char_width;
1942:         if next <= start {
1943:             column = next;
1944:             continue;
1945:         }
1946:         if column < start {
1947:             column = next;
1948:             continue;
1949:         }
1950:         if used + char_width > width {
1951:             break;
1952:         }
1953:         out.push(ch);
1954:         used += char_width;
1955:         column = next;
1956:     }
1957:     out
1958: }
1959: 
1960: fn wrap_text(text: &str, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
1961:     if width == 0 {
1962:         return Vec::new();
1963:     }
1964:     let text = text.replace('\t', "    ");
1965:     let mut lines = Vec::new();
1966:     for raw in text.split('\n') {
1967:         let mut line = String::new();
1968:         let mut used = 0;
1969:         for ch in raw.chars() {
1970:             let char_width = ch.width().unwrap_or(0);
1971:             if used + char_width > width {
1972:                 lines.push(line);
1973:                 line = String::new();
1974:                 used = 0;
1975:             }
1976:             line.push(ch);
1977:             used += char_width;
1978:         }
1979:         lines.push(line);
1980:     }
1981:     lines
1982: }
1983: 
1984: fn install_command() -> &'static str {
1985:     if cfg!(windows) {
1986:         "powershell -c \"irm https://pi.dev/install.ps1 | iex\""
1987:     } else {
1988:         "curl -fsSL https://pi.dev/install.sh | sh"
1989:     }
1990: }

READREADME.md
Read 1/1 file, 113 lines
1: # smol
2: 
3: smol is a small terminal IDE for the pi coding agent. It provides a file tree, a syntax-highlighted editor, a git view, and an agent pane.
4: 
5: ![Screenie](images/screenie.png)
6: 
7: It is built to stay small: the Linux x86_64 release binary is 4.1 MB, and an idle session uses about 5.9 MiB of RSS and 0.2% CPU without a pi subprocess.
8: 
9: ## Requirements
10: 
11: - Rust 1.74 or newer (installed automatically if missing)
12: - git
13: - pi for the agent pane
14: 
15: ## Install
16: 
17: ```bash
18: curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/TheShovel/smol-coder/main/install.sh | bash
19: ```
20: 
21: The script downloads and builds smol, installs it to `~/.local/bin/smol`, and adds that folder to PATH when needed. It installs Rust with rustup if `cargo` is missing.
22: 
23: ## Run
24: 
25: ```bash
26: smol
27: smol path/to/project
28: smol path/to/file
29: ```
30: 
31: Run `smol --help` for all options.
32: 
33: ## Keys
34: 
35: | Key | Action |
36: | --- | --- |
37: | `F6` | Cycle panes |
38: | `Alt+1`, `Alt+2`, `Alt+3` | Focus files, editor, or agent |
39: | `Ctrl+G` | Switch files and git sidebar |
40: | `Ctrl+B` | Hide or show the agent pane |
41: | `Ctrl+S` | Save |
42: | `Ctrl+Z` | Undo |
43: | `Ctrl+Q` | Quit |
44: | `Ctrl+C` | Copy selection |
45: | `Ctrl+X` | Cut selection |
46: | `Ctrl+V` | Paste into the editor or agent prompt |
47: | `Ctrl+N` | Next file |
48: | `Ctrl+P` | Command menu and settings |
49: | `Ctrl+W` | Close file |
50: | `Ctrl+Left`, `Ctrl+Right` | Move by word |
51: | `Ctrl+Home`, `Ctrl+End` | Start or end of file |
52: | `Shift+arrows` | Select text |
53: | `Tab` | Accept a prediction or @ file suggestion, or indent |
54: | `BackTab` | Dedent |
55: | `Enter` | Open a file or diff, send a prompt, or commit |
56: | `Esc` | Stop the agent, close a diff, or leave the git input |
57: 
58: The file tree also supports arrows and `h`, `j`, `k`, `l`. It refreshes automatically when files and folders change, and open buffers close themselves when their file is deleted. Double-click empty space in the tree to create a file. Click files and folders, drag pane dividers to resize, and drag in the editor to select text. Right-click an explorer entry for Open, Open in default app, New file, New folder, Rename, Delete, Copy path, and Copy relative path. Delete asks for confirmation and permanently removes the file or folder. Cut and copy use the local clipboard tool when available, otherwise the terminal's OSC 52 clipboard support; paste reads the system clipboard and falls back to text cut or copied in smol.
59: 
60: Type `@` in the agent input to reference a project file. A list filters paths as you type; use Up/Down and Tab or Enter to insert one.
61: 
62: The command menu groups actions into `Files`, `Settings`, `LLM commands`, and `Git` submenus. The `Git` submenu refreshes status and pushes.
63: 
64: ## pi
65: 
66: smol starts pi as a subprocess in the project folder:
67: 
68: ```bash
69: pi --mode rpc --no-session
70: ```
71: 
72: pi manages authentication, models, and sessions. If it is missing, the agent pane links to its installer. Type `/help` in the agent input for available commands. The command menu's `LLM commands` submenu can explain or review the current file, explain or refactor a selection, add tests, and format the file. Commands that edit a file require it to be saved first.
73: 
74: smol checks context usage after each run and compacts automatically at 95%. pi's built-in auto-compaction is disabled so smol controls the threshold; `/compact` still compacts manually.
75: 
76: Agent reads and edits are highlighted in the editor. The editor follows the active file unless you have unsaved changes there.
77: 
78: ## Git
79: 
80: The git sidebar shows changed files. Click a change to open its diff in the editor.
81: 
82: | Key | Action |
83: | --- | --- |
84: | `c` | Write a commit message |
85: | `p` | Push |
86: | `d` | Discard, press twice to confirm |
87: | `r` | Refresh |
88: | `o` | Open the selected file |
89: 
90: Commits stage all changes. Diffs open in the editor and support keyboard and mouse scrolling.
91: 
92: ## Editing
93: 
94: Inline predictions are local and instant. They learn from open files, a bounded project-wide corpus, saved line transitions, and accepted predictions. The engine uses the current line suffix, the previous line, indentation, and nearby line pairs. On an indented empty line it suggests the next line; `Tab` accepts. Learned lines and transitions are stored in `~/.cache/smol/`. No network request, model download, or pi subprocess is involved.
95: 
96: Binary files and files over 4 MB are not opened in the editor. Unsupported files show a button to open them with the system default application.
97: 
98: ## Development
99: 
100: ```bash
101: cargo build
102: cargo test
103: cargo run -- --force-no-pi
104: ```
105: 
106: ## Uninstall
107: 
108: ```bash
109: rm ~/.local/bin/smol
110: rm -rf ~/.cache/smol
111: ```
112: 
113: Remove the `# smol path` block from your shell startup file if the installer added it.
Exported from Command Code
